Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [114] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoPA是一个用于评估个性化问答的基准，通过挖掘社区-个体偏好分歧来定义六个个性化因素，包含1985个用户配置文件，提供细粒度的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型在问答任务中展现出潜力，但个性化评估仍然是一个关键瓶颈。现有方法主要依赖词法级相似性或人工启发式方法，缺乏足够的数据驱动验证。

Method: 通过挖掘社区-个体偏好分歧（CIPD），即个体选择覆盖共识的情况，提炼出六个关键个性化因素作为评估维度。基于此构建CoPA基准，包含1985个用户配置文件，通过量化模型输出与从交互模式推断的用户特定认知偏好之间的对齐程度进行评估。

Result: CoPA提供了一个比通用指标更全面和更具区分性的个性化问答评估标准，能够进行细粒度的因素级评估。

Conclusion: CoPA通过数据驱动的方法解决了个性化问答评估的瓶颈问题，为评估模型与用户特定认知偏好的对齐提供了更全面的基准。

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [115] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MM-AQA是一个新的多模态基准测试，通过视觉模态依赖性和证据充分性两个维度构建不可回答实例，评估视觉语言模型和多智能体系统的有效弃权能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估范式假设所有问题都可回答，迫使模型总是响应，而实际应用中识别证据不足并弃权对可靠的多模态系统至关重要。文本领域的弃权研究已有进展，但多模态场景下仍缺乏系统探索，现有基准要么忽略不可回答性，要么使用粗糙方法错过真实失败模式。

Method: 提出MM-AQA基准，从可回答实例通过两个轴构建不可回答实例：1)视觉模态依赖性，2)证据充分性。评估了三个前沿视觉语言模型（涵盖闭源和开源模型）和两种多智能体系统架构，共2079个样本。采用标准提示、置信度基线、多智能体系统等多种设置。

Result: 1)标准提示下，视觉语言模型很少弃权，简单置信度基线优于此设置；2)多智能体系统改善弃权但引入准确率-弃权权衡；3)顺序设计与迭代变体相当或更优，表明瓶颈是校准错误而非推理深度；4)模型在图像或文本证据缺失时弃权，但在证据降级或矛盾时尝试调和。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态弃权需要弃权感知训练，而非更好的提示或更多智能体。当前模型在证据完全缺失时能弃权，但面对降级或矛盾证据时仍试图回答，表明需要专门的训练方法来改善校准和证据评估能力。

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [116] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保留优先的梯度合成框架，将LLM遗忘重新定义为非对称双任务问题，通过解耦任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合来缓解遗忘与保留的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型遗忘旨在移除特定知识同时保持通用能力。现有方法面临遗忘与保留之间的权衡问题，需要更有效的解决方案来平衡这两个目标。

Method: 提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，将LLM遗忘重新定义为非对称双任务问题（保留为主目标，遗忘为辅助）。框架包含任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合两个阶段。具体实现了PCGrad变体和SAGO方法，后者通过构造性符号约束合成实现更紧密的对齐。

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准测试中，SAGO方法持续推动帕累托前沿。例如在WMDP Bio（SimNPO+GD）上，目标模型MMLU性能恢复从44.6%（朴素方法）提升到94.0%（+PCGrad），再到96.0%（+SAGO），同时保持相当的遗忘强度。理论分析表明两种变体都确保与保留梯度的非负余弦相似性。

Conclusion: 重塑梯度几何而非重新平衡损失是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键。保留优先的梯度合成框架通过解耦任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合，有效解决了LLM遗忘中的非对称双任务问题。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>


### [117] [Domain Fine-Tuning FinBERT on Finnish Histopathological Reports: Train-Time Signals and Downstream Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14815)
*Rami Luisto,Liisa Petäinen,Tommi Grönholm,Jan Böhm,Maarit Ahtiainen,Tomi Lilja,Ilkka Pölönen,Sami Äyrämö*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在芬兰语医疗文本数据上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调的效果，并尝试通过观察嵌入几何变化来预测领域特定预训练的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗AI领域，获取标注数据集通常需要很长时间，特别是在标签获取方面存在延迟。当标注数据稀缺时，使用未标注数据进行领域微调是NLP分类任务中的常用方法。

Method: 1. 在芬兰语医疗文本数据上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调；2. 通过观察嵌入几何变化来预测领域特定预训练的收益。

Result: 论文报告了芬兰语BERT在医疗文本上领域微调的观察结果，以及通过嵌入几何分析预测领域预训练收益的尝试。

Conclusion: 该研究为医疗AI中数据稀缺情况下的模型优化提供了实用方法，探索了通过嵌入分析预测领域适应效果的可行性。

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [118] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将行为特征标注视为多项技能的集合而非单一任务，通过技能文件驱动的流程评估LLM辅助标注，发现GPT-5.4在保留技能上表现可靠但选择性可行，开源模型存在模式执行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为特征标注难以自动化，因为它需要同时编码多个语言维度。研究将BP标注视为一组标注技能而非单一任务，从这一角度评估LLM辅助的BP标注。

Method: 使用3,134条中文隐喻颜色词衍生词的语料和14个特征的BP模式，实施技能文件驱动的流程，每个特征通过模式文件、决策规则和示例外部定义。两位人工标注者完成两轮仅模式协议，将BP技能分类为可直接操作、可恢复或结构未指定。在相同设置下评估GPT-5.4和三个本地可部署的开源模型。

Result: BP标注在技能层面高度异质：5个技能可直接操作，4个在重点重新标注后可恢复，5个保持结构未指定。GPT-5.4执行保留技能具有实质性可靠性（准确率=0.678，κ=0.665，加权F1=0.695），但这种可行性是选择性的而非全局的。人类和GPT的困难模式在技能层面高度一致（r=0.881），但在实例层面（r=0.016）和词汇项目层面（r=-0.142）不一致。开源模型的失败集中在模式到技能执行问题。

Conclusion: 自动标注应在技能可行性而非任务层面自动化方面进行评估。GPT应被视为独立的第三技能声音而非直接的人类替代品。研究结果表明共享分类法、独立执行的模式。

Abstract: Behavioral Profile (BP) annotation is difficult to automate because it requires simultaneous coding across multiple linguistic dimensions. We treat BP annotation as a bundle of annotation skills rather than a single task and evaluate LLM-assisted BP annotation from this perspective. Using 3,134 concordance lines of 30 Chinese metaphorical color-term derivatives and a 14-feature BP schema, we implement a skill-file-driven pipeline in which each feature is externally defined through schema files, decision rules, and examples. Two human annotators completed a two-round schema-only protocol on a 300-instance validation subset, enabling BP skills to be classified as directly operable, recoverable under focused re-annotation, or structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 and three locally deployable open-source models were then evaluated under the same setup. Results show that BP annotation is highly heterogeneous at the skill level: 5 skills are directly operable, 4 are recoverable after focused re-annotation, and 5 remain structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 executes the retained skills with substantial reliability (accuracy = 0.678, \k{appa} = 0.665, weighted F1 = 0.695), but this feasibility is selective rather than global. Human and GPT difficulty profiles are strongly aligned at the skill level (r = 0.881), but not at the instance level (r = 0.016) or lexical-item level (r = -0.142), a pattern we describe as shared taxonomy, independent execution. Pairwise agreement further suggests that GPT is better understood as an independent third skill voice than as a direct human substitute. Open-source failures are concentrated in schema-to-skill execution problems. These findings suggest that automatic annotation should be evaluated in terms of skill feasibility rather than task-level automation.

</details>


### [119] [Segment-Level Coherence for Robust Harmful Intent Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14865)
*Xuanli He,Bilgehan Sel,Faizan Ali,Jenny Bao,Hoagy Cunningham,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种流式探测方法，通过要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测而非依赖孤立的高分令牌，显著提升了在CBRN领域有害内容检测的准确率，降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在CBRN等高风险领域面临自适应越狱攻击，现有流式探测方法依赖少数高分令牌，在良性上下文中出现敏感术语时容易产生误报，需要更鲁棒的检测机制。

Method: 引入流式探测目标函数，要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测，而非依赖孤立的高分令牌。该方法基于聚合信号而非单令牌线索，并比较了注意力、MLP激活和残差流特征的效果。

Result: 在1%固定误报率下，相比强基线方法，真阳性率相对提升35.55%。AUROC显著提升，即使基线性能已接近饱和（AUROC=97.40%）。注意力或MLP激活的探测始终优于残差流特征。即使对抗微调启用字符级密码，有害意图仍可检测：为基LLM开发的探测器可即插即用应用于这些混淆攻击，AUROC超过98.85%。

Conclusion: 提出的多证据令牌流式探测方法显著提升了CBRN领域有害内容检测的鲁棒性和准确性，能够有效应对自适应越狱攻击，包括字符级混淆技术，展示了即插即用的迁移能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly exposed to adaptive jailbreaking, particularly in high-stakes Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear (CBRN) domains. Although streaming probes enable real-time monitoring, they still make systematic errors. We identify a core issue: existing methods often rely on a few high-scoring tokens, leading to false alarms when sensitive CBRN terms appear in benign contexts. To address this, we introduce a streaming probing objective that requires multiple evidence tokens to consistently support a prediction, rather than relying on isolated spikes. This encourages more robust detection based on aggregated signals instead of single-token cues. At a fixed 1% false-positive rate, our method improves the true-positive rate by 35.55% relative to strong streaming baselines. We further observe substantial gains in AUROC, even when starting from near-saturated baseline performance (AUROC = 97.40%). We also show that probing Attention or MLP activations consistently outperforms residual-stream features. Finally, even when adversarial fine-tuning enables novel character-level ciphers, harmful intent remains detectable: probes developed for the base LLMs can be applied ``plug-and-play'' to these obfuscated attacks, achieving an AUROC of over 98.85%.

</details>


### [120] [Reasoning Dynamics and the Limits of Monitoring Modality Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14888)
*Danae Sánchez Villegas,Samuel Lewis-Lim,Nikolaos Aletras,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了18种视觉语言模型（VLMs）的推理动态，发现模型存在"答案惯性"现象，早期预测在推理步骤中被强化而非修正。虽然推理训练模型显示出更强的纠正行为，但其增益取决于模态条件。模型即使视觉证据充分时仍受误导性文本线索影响，且这种影响在思维链中的可检测性因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）展现出推理能力，但其推理过程如何展开以及如何整合视觉和文本信息仍不清楚。研究者旨在揭示VLMs推理动态的本质，特别是模型在思维链（CoT）过程中如何平衡不同模态信息，以及推理步骤对最终决策的实际贡献。

Method: 研究分析了18个VLMs，涵盖指令微调和推理训练模型，来自两个不同的模型家族。方法包括：1）跟踪思维链过程中的置信度变化；2）测量推理的纠正效果；3）评估中间推理步骤的贡献；4）使用带有误导性文本线索的受控干预实验，分析模型对文本线索的敏感性及其在思维链中的表现。

Result: 研究发现：1）模型存在"答案惯性"，早期预测在推理步骤中被强化而非修正；2）推理训练模型显示出更强的纠正行为，但其增益取决于模态条件（从文本主导到纯视觉设置）；3）模型即使视觉证据充分时仍受误导性文本线索影响；4）这种影响在思维链中的可检测性因模型而异：推理训练模型更可能明确提及线索，但其更长更流畅的思维链可能表面上看似基于视觉而实际遵循文本线索；指令微调模型较少明确提及线索，但其较短的痕迹揭示了与视觉输入的不一致性。

Conclusion: 思维链仅提供了不同模态如何驱动VLM决策的部分视图，这对多模态系统的透明度和安全性具有重要意义。研究揭示了VLMs推理过程的局限性，表明需要更深入的方法来理解和评估多模态模型的真实推理机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) offer reasoning capabilities, yet how these unfold and integrate visual and textual information remains unclear. We analyze reasoning dynamics in 18 VLMs covering instruction-tuned and reasoning-trained models from two different model families. We track confidence over Chain-of-Thought (CoT), measure the corrective effect of reasoning, and evaluate the contribution of intermediate reasoning steps. We find that models are prone to answer inertia, in which early commitments to a prediction are reinforced, rather than revised during reasoning steps. While reasoning-trained models show stronger corrective behavior, their gains depend on modality conditions, from text-dominant to vision-only settings. Using controlled interventions with misleading textual cues, we show that models are consistently influenced by these cues even when visual evidence is sufficient, and assess whether this influence is recoverable from CoT. Although this influence can appear in the CoT, its detectability varies across models and depends on what is being monitored. Reasoning-trained models are more likely to explicitly refer to the cues, but their longer and fluent CoTs can still appear visually grounded while actually following textual cues, obscuring modality reliance. In contrast, instruction-tuned models refer to the cues less explicitly, but their shorter traces reveal inconsistencies with the visual input. Taken together, these findings indicate that CoT provides only a partial view of how different modalities drive VLM decisions, with important implications for the transparency and safety of multimodal systems.

</details>


### [121] [Comparison of Modern Multilingual Text Embedding Techniques for Hate Speech Detection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14907)
*Evaldas Vaiciukynas,Paulius Danenas,Linas Ablonskis,Algirdas Sukys,Edgaras Dambrauskas,Voldemaras Zitkus,Rita Butkiene,Rimantas Butleris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了多语言句子嵌入模型在立陶宛语、俄语和英语仇恨言论检测中的性能，引入了LtHate新语料库，比较了六种嵌入模型和两种分类方法，发现监督分类显著优于异常检测，PCA压缩在监督场景下保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论和辱骂性语言在多语言环境特别是低资源语言（如立陶宛语）中日益严重，需要有效的自动检测方法。研究旨在评估现代多语言句子嵌入模型在仇恨言论检测中的能力，并探索下游建模选择和特征维度对性能的影响。

Method: 1) 引入LtHate立陶宛语仇恨言论语料库；2) 在LtHate、RuToxic和EnSuperset三个数据集上对六种多语言编码器进行基准测试；3) 对每种嵌入训练单类HBOS异常检测器和双类CatBoost分类器；4) 使用和不使用PCA压缩到64维特征向量进行对比实验。

Result: 监督模型在所有数据集上显著优于异常检测：立陶宛语最佳配置达到80.96%准确率和0.887 AUC ROC（jina），俄语92.19%准确率和0.978 AUC ROC（e5），英语77.21%准确率和0.859 AUC ROC（e5+PCA）。PCA压缩在监督场景下几乎保持全部判别能力，但对无监督异常检测有负面影响。

Conclusion: 现代多语言句子嵌入结合梯度提升决策树为多语言仇恨言论检测提供了稳健的软计算方法。监督分类方法明显优于异常检测，PCA压缩在监督场景下是有效的降维策略，为低资源语言仇恨言论检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Online hate speech and abusive language pose a growing challenge for content moderation, especially in multilingual settings and for low-resource languages such as Lithuanian. This paper investigates to what extent modern multilingual sentence embedding models can support accurate hate speech detection in Lithuanian, Russian, and English, and how their performance depends on downstream modeling choices and feature dimensionality. We introduce LtHate, a new Lithuanian hate speech corpus derived from news portals and social networks, and benchmark six modern multilingual encoders (potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5) on LtHate, RuToxic, and EnSuperset using a unified Python pipeline. For each embedding, we train both a one class HBOS anomaly detector and a two class CatBoost classifier, with and without principal component analysis (PCA) compression to 64-dimensional feature vectors. Across all datasets, two class supervised models consistently and substantially outperform one class anomaly detection, with the best configurations achieving up to 80.96% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.887 in Lithuanian (jina), 92.19% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.978 in Russian (e5), and 77.21% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.859 in English (e5 with PCA). PCA compression preserves almost all discriminative power in the supervised setting, while showing some negative impact for the unsupervised anomaly detection case. These results demonstrate how modern multilingual sentence embeddings combined with gradient boosted decision trees provide robust soft-computing solutions for multilingual hate speech detection applications.

</details>


### [122] [IE as Cache: Information Extraction Enhanced Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14930)
*Hang Lv,Sheng Liang,Hongchao Gu,Wei Guo,Defu Lian,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IE-as-Cache框架，将信息抽取重新定位为认知缓存，通过查询驱动抽取和缓存感知推理增强智能体推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息抽取仅作为终端目标，抽取结果孤立使用而非在多步推理中维护复用，限制了其在下游理解与推理中的潜力

Method: 受计算机分层内存启发，结合查询驱动抽取与缓存感知推理，动态维护紧凑中间信息并过滤噪声

Result: 在多样化LLMs的挑战性基准测试中，推理准确性显著提升

Conclusion: 信息抽取可有效重用作可复用认知资源，为IE下游应用研究提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Information Extraction aims to distill structured, decision-relevant information from unstructured text, serving as a foundation for downstream understanding and reasoning. However, it is traditionally treated merely as a terminal objective: once extracted, the resulting structure is often consumed in isolation rather than maintained and reused during multi-step inference. Moving beyond this, we propose \textit{IE-as-Cache}, a framework that repurposes IE as a cognitive cache to enhance agentic reasoning. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical computer memory, our approach combines query-driven extraction with cache-aware reasoning to dynamically maintain compact intermediate information and filter noise. Experiments on challenging benchmarks across diverse LLMs demonstrate significant improvements in reasoning accuracy, indicating that IE can be effectively repurposed as a reusable cognitive resource and offering a promising direction for future research on downstream uses of IE.

</details>


### [123] [XQ-MEval: A Dataset with Cross-lingual Parallel Quality for Benchmarking Translation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14934)
*Jingxuan Liu,Zhi Qu,Jin Tei,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XQ-MEval：一个用于评估翻译指标的半自动构建数据集，通过注入可控错误创建平行质量实例，揭示了跨语言评分偏差问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言翻译系统评估通常采用跨语言平均指标分数的方法，但这种方法存在可疑之处，因为指标可能存在跨语言评分偏差——同等质量的翻译在不同语言中可能获得不同分数。由于缺乏提供跨语言平行质量实例的基准数据集，且专家标注不现实，这一问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 提出XQ-MEval数据集构建方法：1）在黄金翻译中自动注入MQM定义的错误；2）由母语者进行可靠性过滤；3）合并错误生成具有可控质量的伪翻译；4）将伪翻译与对应的源文本和参考翻译组成三元组用于评估翻译指标。数据集覆盖9个翻译方向。

Result: 使用XQ-MEval对9个代表性指标进行实验，揭示了平均评分与人工判断之间的不一致性，首次提供了跨语言评分偏差的经验证据。基于XQ-MEval提出的归一化策略能够对齐跨语言分数分布，提高了多语言指标评估的公平性和可靠性。

Conclusion: XQ-MEval为系统研究翻译指标的跨语言评分偏差提供了首个基准数据集，揭示了当前评估方法的局限性，并提出了有效的归一化解决方案，对构建更公平可靠的多语言翻译评估系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are essential for building multilingual translation systems. The common practice of evaluating these systems is averaging metric scores across languages, yet this is suspicious since metrics may suffer from cross-lingual scoring bias, where translations of equal quality receive different scores across languages. This problem has not been systematically studied because no benchmark exists that provides parallel-quality instances across languages, and expert annotation is not realistic. In this work, we propose XQ-MEval, a semi-automatically built dataset covering nine translation directions, to benchmark translation metrics. Specifically, we inject MQM-defined errors into gold translations automatically, filter them by native speakers for reliability, and merge errors to generate pseudo translations with controllable quality. These pseudo translations are then paired with corresponding sources and references to form triplets used in assessing the qualities of translation metrics. Using XQ-MEval, our experiments on nine representative metrics reveal the inconsistency between averaging and human judgment and provide the first empirical evidence of cross-lingual scoring bias. Finally, we propose a normalization strategy derived from XQ-MEval that aligns score distributions across languages, improving the fairness and reliability of multilingual metric evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Text2Arch: A Dataset for Generating Scientific Architecture Diagrams from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14941)
*Shivank Garg,Sankalp Mittal,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种从文本自动生成科学架构图的系统，通过构建包含图像、文本描述和DOT代码的数据集，并微调小型语言模型，实现了与GPT-4o相当的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅通过文本传达复杂系统设计或科学过程效率低下且容易产生歧义。自动从文本生成高语义保真度的科学架构图在多个应用场景中具有重要价值，如企业架构可视化、AI驱动的软件设计和教育内容创建。

Method: 1. 构建了包含科学架构图像、对应文本描述和DOT代码表示的完整数据集；2. 利用该数据集微调了一系列小型语言模型；3. 同时使用GPT-4o进行上下文学习；4. 通过语言模型对输入文本进行语义理解，生成中间代码，再处理生成高保真架构图。

Result: 提出的模型显著优于现有基线模型（如DiagramAgent），并且与基于上下文学习的GPT-4o生成效果相当。代码、数据和模型均已公开。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了缺乏大规模开放数据集和有效开放模型的空白，通过构建数据集和开发模型，实现了从文本到科学架构图的高质量自动生成，为相关应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Communicating complex system designs or scientific processes through text alone is inefficient and prone to ambiguity. A system that automatically generates scientific architecture diagrams from text with high semantic fidelity can be useful in multiple applications like enterprise architecture visualization, AI-driven software design, and educational content creation. Hence, in this paper, we focus on leveraging language models to perform semantic understanding of the input text description to generate intermediate code that can be processed to generate high-fidelity architecture diagrams. Unfortunately, no clean large-scale open-access dataset exists, implying lack of any effective open models for this task. Hence, we contribute a comprehensive dataset, \system, comprising scientific architecture images, their corresponding textual descriptions, and associated DOT code representations. Leveraging this resource, we fine-tune a suite of small language models, and also perform in-context learning using GPT-4o. Through extensive experimentation, we show that \system{} models significantly outperform existing baseline models like DiagramAgent and perform at par with in-context learning-based generations from GPT-4o. We make the code, data and models publicly available.

</details>


### [125] [Explain the Flag: Contextualizing Hate Speech Beyond Censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14970)
*Jason Liartis,Eirini Kaldeli,Lambrini Gyftokosta,Eleftherios Chelioudakis,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LLM与定制词汇表的混合方法，用于检测和解释英语、法语、希腊语中的仇恨言论，通过两个互补管道生成有根据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仇恨言论检测系统大多侧重于审查或删除，缺乏透明度并限制言论自由，且无法解释内容为何有害。需要更透明、可问责且信息丰富的检测方法。

Method: 开发混合方法：结合大型语言模型与三个新创建的定制词汇表。采用两个互补管道：1) 使用词汇表检测和消歧问题术语；2) 利用LLM作为上下文感知的群体目标内容评估器。将输出融合为有根据的解释。

Result: 人类评估显示该混合方法准确度高，解释质量优秀，优于仅使用LLM的基线方法。系统能有效检测与身份特征相关的固有贬义表达和直接针对群体的内容。

Conclusion: 混合方法为仇恨言论检测提供了更透明、可解释的解决方案，通过生成有根据的解释来澄清内容被标记的原因，平衡了检测效果与透明度需求。

Abstract: Hate, derogatory, and offensive speech remains a persistent challenge in online platforms and public discourse. While automated detection systems are widely used, most focus on censorship or removal, raising concerns for transparency and freedom of expression, and limiting opportunities to explain why content is harmful. To address these issues, explanatory approaches have emerged as a promising solution, aiming to make hate speech detection more transparent, accountable, and informative. In this paper, we present a hybrid approach that combines Large Language Models (LLMs) with three newly created and curated vocabularies to detect and explain hate speech in English, French, and Greek. Our system captures both inherently derogatory expressions tied to identity characteristics and direct group-targeted content through two complementary pipelines: one that detects and disambiguates problematic terms using the curated vocabularies, and one that leverages LLMs as context-aware evaluators of group-targeting content. The outputs are fused into grounded explanations that clarify why content is flagged. Human evaluation shows that our hybrid approach is accurate, with high-quality explanations, outperforming LLM-only baselines.

</details>


### [126] [IUQ: Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification for Long-Form Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15109)
*Haozhi Fan,Jinhao Duan,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ)框架，通过样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来量化大语言模型长文本生成的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型快速发展，但其生成内容的不确定性量化仍具挑战。现有方法多限于短文本或受限答案集，而实际应用需要长文本自由生成。主要困难在于模型常生成语义连贯但事实不准确的响应，且语义多面、语言结构复杂。

Method: 提出IUQ框架，采用"询问-响应"范式，利用样本间一致性（不同样本间的一致性）和样本内忠实性（模型对其生成内容的忠实度）来量化长文本输出的不确定性。该方法提供声明级不确定性和模型忠实度的可靠度量。

Result: 在不同模型家族和模型规模上的实验结果表明，IUQ在两个广泛使用的长文本生成数据集上表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: IUQ框架能有效量化大语言模型长文本生成的不确定性，通过结合样本间一致性和样本内忠实性，为实际应用中的长文本生成提供了可靠的不确定性度量方法。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), uncertainty quantification in LLM generation is a persistent challenge. Although recent approaches have achieved strong performance by restricting LLMs to produce short or constrained answer sets, many real-world applications require long-form and free-form text generation. A key difficulty in this setting is that LLMs often produce responses that are semantically coherent yet factually inaccurate, while the underlying semantics are multifaceted and the linguistic structure is complex. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ), a novel framework that leverages inter-sample consistency and intra-sample faithfulness to quantify the uncertainty in long-form LLM outputs. By utilizing an interrogate-then-respond paradigm, our method provides reliable measures of claim-level uncertainty and the model's faithfulness. Experimental results across diverse model families and model sizes demonstrate the superior performance of IUQ over two widely used long-form generation datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/louisfanhz/IUQ.

</details>


### [127] [DiscoTrace: Representing and Comparing Answering Strategies of Humans and LLMs in Information-Seeking Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15140)
*Neha Srikanth,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoTrace是一种识别回答者回应信息寻求问题时使用的修辞策略的方法，通过话语行为序列和问题解释分析，发现人类社区具有多样化的回答构建偏好，而LLMs缺乏修辞多样性且倾向于过度回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解不同人类社区在回答信息寻求问题时使用的修辞策略差异，并对比分析大型语言模型（LLMs）的回答模式，以揭示LLMs在修辞多样性方面的局限性。

Method: 提出DiscoTrace方法，将回答表示为问题相关话语行为序列，结合原始问题的解释，基于修辞结构理论解析进行标注。该方法应用于九个不同人类社区的答案分析。

Result: 人类社区展现出多样化的回答构建偏好，而LLMs即使被提示模仿特定人类社区回答指南，也未能表现出修辞多样性。LLMs系统性地倾向于广度，回答了人类回答者选择不涉及的问题解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为开发考虑上下文多种策略的实用型LLM回答者提供指导，促进LLMs在问答任务中更好地理解和适应不同社区的修辞偏好。

Abstract: We introduce DiscoTrace, a method to identify the rhetorical strategies that answerers use when responding to information-seeking questions. DiscoTrace represents answers as a sequence of question-related discourse acts paired with interpretations of the original question, annotated on top of rhetorical structure theory parses. Applying DiscoTrace to answers from nine different human communities reveals that communities have diverse preferences for answer construction. In contrast, LLMs do not exhibit rhetorical diversity in their answers, even when prompted to mimic specific human community answering guidelines. LLMs also systematically opt for breadth, addressing interpretations of questions that human answerers choose not to address. Our findings can guide the development of pragmatic LLM answerers that consider a range of strategies informed by context in QA.

</details>


### [128] [Compressing Sequences in the Latent Embedding Space: $K$-Token Merging for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15153)
*Zihao Xu,John Harvill,Ziwei Fan,Yizhou Sun,Hao Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: K-Token Merging：一种在潜在嵌入空间压缩长提示的框架，通过将连续的K个令牌嵌入合并为单个嵌入，实现高达75%的输入长度减少，性能损失最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型处理长提示时计算和内存成本高昂，因为完全自注意力随输入长度呈二次方增长。现有的提示压缩方法主要在令牌空间操作，忽视了潜在嵌入空间的低效性。

Method: 提出K-Token Merging框架：使用轻量级编码器将每个连续的K个令牌嵌入块合并为单个嵌入，压缩后的序列由LoRA适配的LLM处理，而生成仍在原始词汇表中进行。

Result: 在结构推理（Textualized Tree）、情感分类（Amazon Reviews）和代码编辑（CommitPackFT）任务上的实验表明，K-Token Merging在性能与压缩比之间达到帕累托前沿，实现高达75%的输入长度减少，性能下降最小。

Conclusion: K-Token Merging通过在潜在嵌入空间进行压缩，有效解决了LLM处理长提示时的计算效率问题，为长上下文处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur significant computational and memory costs when processing long prompts, as full self-attention scales quadratically with input length. Token compression aims to address this challenge by reducing the number of tokens representing inputs. However, existing prompt-compression approaches primarily operate in token space and overlook inefficiencies in the latent embedding space. In this paper, we propose K-Token Merging, a latent-space compression framework that merges each contiguous block of K token embeddings into a single embedding via a lightweight encoder. The compressed sequence is processed by a LoRA-adapted LLM, while generation remains in the original vocabulary. Experiments on structural reasoning (Textualized Tree), sentiment classification (Amazon Reviews), and code editing (CommitPackFT) show that K-Token Merging lies on the Pareto frontier of performance vs. compression, achieving up to 75% input length reduction with minimal performance degradation.

</details>


### [129] [Fabricator or dynamic translator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15165)
*Lisa Vasileva,Karin Sim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了大型语言模型在机器翻译中的过度生成现象，分析了其不同于传统神经机器翻译的特点，并提出了在商业环境中检测和分类这些过度生成的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在机器翻译中表现出色，但由于其生成性质，有时会产生各种形式的过度生成。这些过度生成与传统神经机器翻译中的"神经胡言"不同，范围从LLM自我解释、风险性虚构到适当的解释，其中LLM能够像人类翻译者一样工作，为目标受众提供更好的理解。检测和确定这些过度生成的确切性质是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 论文详细介绍了在商业环境中探索的不同策略，包括检测和分类LLM在翻译中过度生成的方法。这些策略旨在区分不同类型的过度生成，如自我解释、风险性虚构和适当的解释性翻译。

Result: 论文展示了在商业环境中应用这些策略的结果，提供了关于如何有效识别和处理LLM翻译中过度生成现象的实证发现。

Conclusion: LLM在机器翻译中的过度生成是一个复杂现象，需要专门的检测和分类方法。在商业环境中，正确处理这些过度生成对于确保翻译质量和可靠性至关重要，同时也能利用LLM的解释能力来增强目标受众的理解。

Abstract: LLMs are proving to be adept at machine translation although due to their generative nature they may at times overgenerate in various ways. These overgenerations are different from the neurobabble seen in NMT and range from LLM self-explanations, to risky confabulations, to appropriate explanations, where the LLM is able to act as a human translator would, enabling greater comprehension for the target audience. Detecting and determining the exact nature of the overgenerations is a challenging task. We detail different strategies we have explored for our work in a commercial setting, and present our results.

</details>


### [130] [From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15244)
*Kiran Purohit,Ramasuri Narayanam,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecGuard是一种验证感知的推测解码框架，通过模型内部信号进行步骤级验证，提高推理准确性和效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码的token中心特性导致错误传播，现有方法使用外部奖励模型但带来额外延迟和计算开销，且泛化性受限

Method: 提出SpecGuard框架：1) 采样多个草稿候选并选择最一致步骤；2) 使用两种轻量级模型内部信号验证：基于注意力的grounding分数和基于log-probability的置信度分数；3) 联合判断是否接受步骤或使用目标模型重新计算

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，SpecGuard将准确率提高3.6%，同时降低延迟约11%，优于传统推测解码和奖励引导的推测解码

Conclusion: SpecGuard通过仅使用模型内部信号的步骤级验证，有效解决了推测解码中的错误传播问题，在保持效率的同时显著提升准确性

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAGC（能量感知梯度协调器）解决GCD中梯度纠缠问题，通过锚定梯度对齐和能量感知弹性投影提升已知类和新类分离性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义类别发现（GCD）利用标记数据对未知类别的未标记样本进行分类，但现有方法中监督和无监督目标的联合优化存在梯度纠缠问题，这会扭曲监督梯度、削弱已知类间区分度，并导致已知类与新类表示子空间重叠，降低新类别的可分性

Method: 提出EAGC（能量感知梯度协调器），包含两个组件：1）基于锚点的梯度对齐（AGA）-引入参考模型锚定标记样本的梯度方向，保护已知类判别结构免受未标记梯度干扰；2）能量感知弹性投影（EEP）-将未标记梯度软投影到已知类子空间的补空间，基于能量系数自适应缩放投影，减少子空间重叠同时不抑制可能属于已知类的未标记样本

Result: 实验表明EAGC能持续提升现有方法性能，并建立了新的最先进结果

Conclusion: EAGC通过梯度级模块显式调节优化过程，有效解决了GCD中的梯度纠缠问题，提高了已知类和新类的分离性，是一种即插即用的有效解决方案

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [132] [Towards Verified and Targeted Explanations through Formal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14209)
*Hanchen David Wang,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Preston K. Robinette,Ipek Oguz,Taylor T. Johnson,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViTaX：首个提供形式化保证的针对性解释框架，通过目标ε鲁棒性认证模型对特定关键替代类别的抵抗力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法存在局限：启发式归因方法缺乏数学保证，形式化方法分析最近边界但非针对性。在安全关键系统中，不同误分类后果差异巨大，需要针对特定关键替代类别提供有形式化保证的解释。

Method: ViTaX框架：1) 识别对y→t转换最敏感的最小特征子集；2) 应用形式可达性分析，保证在ε扰动下这些特征不会导致分类翻转到目标类别t。通过目标ε鲁棒性形式化认证特征子集对特定目标类别的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MNIST、GTSRB、EMNIST和TaxiNet数据集上评估，相比现有方法，ViTaX在最小解释基数下实现超过30%的保真度提升，是首个提供形式化保证的针对性解释方法。

Conclusion: ViTaX填补了XAI领域的关键空白，为安全关键应用提供了具有数学保证的针对性半事实解释，使利益相关者能够验证模型对特定高风险误分类的抵抗力。

Abstract: As deep neural networks are deployed in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medical diagnosis, stakeholders need explanations that are interpretable but also trustworthy with formal guarantees. Existing XAI methods fall short: heuristic attribution techniques (e.g., LIME, Integrated Gradients) highlight influential features but offer no mathematical guarantees about decision boundaries, while formal methods verify robustness yet remain untargeted, analyzing the nearest boundary regardless of whether it represents a critical risk. In safety-critical systems, not all misclassifications carry equal consequences; confusing a "Stop" sign for a "60 kph" sign is far more dangerous than confusing it with a "No Passing" sign. We introduce ViTaX (Verified and Targeted Explanations), a formal XAI framework that generates targeted semifactual explanations with mathematical guarantees. For a given input (class y) and a user-specified critical alternative (class t), ViTaX: (1) identifies the minimal feature subset most sensitive to the y->t transition, and (2) applies formal reachability analysis to guarantee that perturbing these features by epsilon cannot flip the classification to t. We formalize this through Targeted epsilon-Robustness, certifying whether a feature subset remains robust under perturbation toward a specific target class. ViTaX is the first method to provide formally guaranteed explanations of a model's resilience against user-identified alternatives. Evaluations on MNIST, GTSRB, EMNIST, and TaxiNet demonstrate over 30% fidelity improvement with minimal explanation cardinality.

</details>


### [133] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了金融欺诈检测AI模型的可解释性质量，提出了基于SHAP的自适应集成方法SGAE，并在完整数据集上比较了三种架构，将结果映射到监管合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年给美国机构造成超过320亿美元损失。虽然AI欺诈检测工具日益先进，但在实际应用中面临主要障碍：许多模型是黑箱，无法提供OCC Bulletin 2011-12和Federal Reserve SR 11-7等法规要求的透明、可审计的解释。

Method: 1) 全面评估解释质量，包括忠实性（在k=5、10、15时的充分性和全面性）和稳定性（30个bootstrap样本的Kendall's W）；2) 提出SHAP引导的自适应集成（SGAE），基于SHAP归因一致性动态调整每笔交易的集成权重；3) 在完整的590,540笔交易IEEE-CIS数据集上对LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE三种架构进行完整评估。

Result: XGBoost配合TreeExplainer达到近乎完美的稳定性（W=0.9912），而LSTM配合DeepExplainer表现较弱（W=0.4962）。SGAE在所有测试模型中达到最高的AUC-ROC（0.8837保留集；0.9245交叉验证）。GNN-GraphSAGE在完整数据集上表现最佳，AUC-ROC 0.9248，F1=0.6013。所有结果直接映射到OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 该研究为金融欺诈检测提供了可解释AI的全面评估框架，提出的SGAE方法在保持高性能的同时满足监管合规要求，GNN-GraphSAGE在完整数据集上表现最优，为金融机构部署符合监管要求的AI欺诈检测系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [134] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOPCELL：基于大语言模型的晶体管拓扑优化框架，将高维拓扑探索重构为生成任务，在2nm先进工艺节点上显著优于基础模型，在7nm库生成任务中实现85.91倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体管拓扑优化是标准单元设计的关键步骤，直接影响扩散共享效率和布线可行性。随着先进节点电路复杂度增加，传统穷举搜索方法计算不可行，需要可扩展的解决方案

Method: 提出TOPCELL框架，将高维拓扑探索重构为基于大语言模型的生成任务，使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）微调模型，使其拓扑优化策略与逻辑（电路）和空间（布局）约束对齐

Result: 在针对2nm先进工艺节点的工业流程中，TOPCELL在发现可布线、物理感知拓扑方面显著优于基础模型。在7nm库生成任务中集成到最先进自动化流程时，表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，匹配穷举求解器的布局质量，同时实现85.91倍加速

Conclusion: TOPCELL为晶体管拓扑优化提供了可扩展的解决方案，通过大语言模型和GRPO微调，在保持布局质量的同时大幅提升优化效率，解决了先进节点中传统方法计算不可行的问题

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [135] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个研究在显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习，提出RHC-UCRL算法，通过分离认知和偶然不确定性，实现次线性遗憾和安全约束违反保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策系统中，状态转移不仅受智能体动作影响，还受外部不可控因素（竞争智能体、环境扰动、战略对手）影响。忽略这些因素可能导致在孤立环境中最优的策略在部署时灾难性失败，特别是在必须满足安全约束的场景中。现有方法要么假设智能体是状态演变的唯一驱动因素，要么通过转移核的分布鲁棒性处理，但未显式建模智能体与外部因素的战略交互。

Method: 将外部因素建模为对抗策略π̄，共同决定状态转移。提出Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL (RHC-UCRL)算法，这是一种基于模型的方法，同时保持对智能体和对抗策略的乐观估计，显式分离认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: RHC-UCRL算法实现了次线性遗憾和约束违反保证，为在显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究在显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习的工作，提出的RHC-UCRL算法通过建模对抗策略并分离不确定性类型，为安全关键环境中的鲁棒决策提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [136] [Awakening Dormant Experts:Counterfactual Routing to Mitigate MoE Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14246)
*Wentao Hu,Yanbo Zhai,Xiaohui Hu,Mingkuan Zhao,Shanhong yu,Xue Liu,Kaidong Yu,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)框架，通过训练无关的推理方法唤醒稀疏MoE模型中休眠的专家，提升长尾知识处理能力而不增加计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE模型在处理长尾知识时容易产生幻觉，主要原因是静态Top-k路由倾向于高频模式而非罕见事实关联，导致拥有关键长尾知识的"专家专家"被分配低门控分数而"休眠"

Method: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练无关推理框架，结合层间扰动分析和Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI)指标，动态将计算资源从语法主导层转移到知识密集型层，通过虚拟消融检索因果决定性专家，保持总激活数不变

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FACTOR和TriviaQA上的实验表明，CoR平均提高事实准确性3.1%，不增加推理预算，相比静态缩放策略建立了更优的帕累托前沿

Conclusion: CoR框架有效解决了稀疏MoE模型中静态路由导致的专家休眠问题，通过动态资源分配唤醒关键专家，显著提升长尾知识处理能力，为高效推理提供了新方向

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable scalability, yet they remain vulnerable to hallucinations, particularly when processing long-tail knowledge. We identify that this fragility stems from static Top-$k$ routing: routers tend to favor high-frequency patterns over rare factual associations. Consequently, ``specialist experts'' possessing critical long-tail knowledge are often assigned low gating scores and remain ``dormant'' -- under-prioritized for specific tokens despite their proven causal importance on other inputs. To address this, we propose Counterfactual Routing (CoR), a training-free inference framework designed to awaken these dormant experts. CoR integrates layer-wise perturbation analysis with the Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI) metric to dynamically shift computational resources from syntax-dominant to knowledge-intensive layers while maintaining a constant total activation count, effectively retrieving causally decisive experts via virtual ablation. Extensive experiments on TruthfulQA, FACTOR, and TriviaQA demonstrate that CoR improves factual accuracy by 3.1\% on average without increasing the inference budget, establishing a superior Pareto frontier compared to static scaling strategies.

</details>


### [137] [Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA):A Unified Framework for Scale-Invariant Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14249)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPCA是一个基于广义特征问题的尺度不变表示学习统一框架，通过度量矩阵M控制表示几何，连接了从标准PCA到输出白化的连续谱偏置控制，并揭示了多种自监督学习目标的几何统一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法缺乏统一的几何框架，特别是尺度不变性在谱偏置控制中的角色不明确。需要建立一个能够统一标准PCA、白化方法以及自监督学习目标的框架，以揭示它们之间的几何联系和差异。

Method: 提出Metric-Aware PCA (MAPCA)框架，基于广义特征问题max Tr(W^T Σ W) subject to W^T M W = I，其中M是对称正定度量矩阵。引入规范β族M(β)=Σ^β，β∈[0,1]提供从标准PCA(β=0)到输出白化(β=1)的连续谱偏置控制。对角度量M=D=diag(Σ)恢复不变PCA(IPCA)。

Result: MAPCA统一了多种表示学习方法：Barlow Twins和ZCA白化对应β=1；VICReg的方差项对应对角度量；W-MSE对应M=Σ^{-1}(β=-1)，处于谱压缩范围之外且与Barlow Twins方向相反。证明了尺度不变性当且仅当度量变换为M_tilde=CMC时成立，IPCA精确满足此条件而β族在中间值不满足。

Conclusion: MAPCA提供了一个统一的几何语言，揭示了自监督学习目标之间的深层联系和差异，特别是输入白化与输出白化的区别在损失函数层面不可见，只有在MAPCA框架下才变得精确。该框架为尺度不变表示学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA), a unified framework for scale-invariant representation learning based on the generalised eigenproblem max Tr(W^T Sigma W) subject to W^T M W = I, where M is a symmetric positive definite metric matrix. The choice of M determines the representation geometry. The canonical beta-family M(beta) = Sigma^beta, beta in [0,1], provides continuous spectral bias control between standard PCA (beta=0) and output whitening (beta=1), with condition number kappa(beta) = (lambda_1/lambda_p)^(1-beta) decreasing monotonically to isotropy. The diagonal metric M = D = diag(Sigma) recovers Invariant PCA (IPCA), a method rooted in Frisch (1928) diagonal regression, as a distinct member of the broader framework. We prove that scale invariance holds if and only if the metric transforms as M_tilde = CMC under rescaling C, a condition satisfied exactly by IPCA but not by the general beta-family at intermediate values.
  Beyond its classical interpretation, MAPCA provides a geometric language that unifies several self-supervised learning objectives. Barlow Twins and ZCA whitening correspond to beta=1 (output whitening); VICReg's variance term corresponds to the diagonal metric. A key finding is that W-MSE, despite being described as a whitening-based method, corresponds to M = Sigma^{-1} (beta = -1), outside the spectral compression range entirely and in the opposite spectral direction to Barlow Twins. This distinction between input and output whitening is invisible at the level of loss functions and becomes precise only within the MAPCA framework.

</details>


### [138] [GUI-Perturbed: Domain Randomization Reveals Systematic Brittleness in GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14262)
*Yangyue Wang,Harshvardhan Sikka,Yash Mathur,Tony Zhou,Jinu Nyachhyon,Pranav Guruprasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUI-Perturbed框架通过独立扰动视觉场景和指令来评估GUI基础模型的鲁棒性，发现空间推理指令导致系统性精度崩溃，70%浏览器缩放产生显著性能下降，LoRA微调反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基础模型在标准基准测试中报告超过85%的准确率，但当指令需要空间推理而非直接元素命名时，性能下降27-56个百分点。当前基准测试的局限性在于每个截图仅用单一固定指令评估一次，无法全面衡量模型鲁棒性。

Method: 引入GUI-Perturbed框架，通过独立变化视觉场景和指令来测量基础模型的鲁棒性。该方法创建受控扰动，评估三个来自相同架构谱系的7B模型，分析空间推理指令、视觉扰动（如70%浏览器缩放）以及LoRA微调对性能的影响。

Result: 关系性指令导致所有模型的系统性精度崩溃；70%浏览器缩放产生统计显著的性能下降；使用增强数据进行rank-8 LoRA微调反而降低性能而非提升。通过独立扰动轴，GUI-Perturbed能够隔离特定能力轴（空间推理、视觉鲁棒性、推理校准）的影响。

Conclusion: GUI-Perturbed框架提供了聚合基准测试无法提供的诊断信号，能够识别模型在空间推理、视觉鲁棒性和推理校准方面的具体弱点。作者发布了数据集、增强管道和微调模型，为GUI基础模型的鲁棒性评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: GUI grounding models report over 85% accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet drop 27-56 percentage points when instructions require spatial reasoning rather than direct element naming. Current benchmarks miss this because they evaluate each screenshot once with a single fixed instruction. We introduce GUI-Perturbed, a controlled perturbation framework that independently varies visual scenes and instructions to measure grounding robustness. Evaluating three 7B models from the same architecture lineage, we find that relational instructions cause systematic accuracy collapse across all models, a 70% browser zoom produces statistically significant degradation, and rank-8 LoRA fine-tuning with augmented data degrades performance rather than improving it. By perturbing along independent axes, GUI-Perturbed isolates which specific capability axes are affected-spatial reasoning, visual robustness, reasoning calibration-providing diagnostic signal that aggregate benchmarks cannot. We release the dataset, augmentation pipeline, and a fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [139] [Reinforcement Learning via Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14265)
*Haoran Xu,Kaiwen Hu,Somayeh Sojoudi,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VGF提出了一种基于最优传输的可扩展行为正则化强化学习新范式，通过离散梯度流将参考分布映射到价值诱导的最优策略分布，无需显式策略参数化即可实现自适应正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为正则化RL方法存在局限性：基于重参数化策略梯度的方法难以扩展到大型生成模型，而拒绝采样方法在试图超越行为支持时可能过于保守。需要一种既可扩展又能灵活超越参考分布的方法。

Method: 将行为正则化RL建模为最优传输问题，将参考分布映射到价值诱导的最优策略分布。通过离散梯度流求解，其中价值梯度引导从参考分布初始化的粒子。通过控制传输预算实现隐式正则化，无需显式策略参数化。

Result: 在离线RL基准测试（D4RL、OGBench）和LLM RL任务上显著优于现有方法，取得了最先进的结果。方法支持通过调整传输预算实现自适应测试时缩放。

Conclusion: VGF为行为正则化RL提供了一个可扩展、灵活且表达性强的新范式，通过最优传输框架有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在多个领域实现了性能突破。

Abstract: We study behavior-regularized reinforcement learning (RL), where regularization toward a reference distribution (the dataset in offline RL or the base model in LLM RL finetuning) is essential to prevent value over-optimization caused by erroneous out-of-distribution extrapolation. Existing methods either rely on reparameterized policy gradient, which are difficult to scale to large generative models, or on reject sampling, which can be overly conservative when attempting to move beyond the behavior support. In this paper, we propose Value Gradient Flow (VGF), a scalable new paradigm for behavior-regularized RL. VGF casts behavior-regularized RL as an optimal transport problem that maps the reference distribution to the value-induced optimal policy distribution. We solve this transport problem via discrete gradient flow, where value gradients guide particles initialized from the reference distribution. Our analysis shows that VGF imposes regularization implicitly by controlling the transport budget. VGF eliminates explicit policy parameterization while remaining expressive and flexible, this enables adaptive test-time scaling by adjusting the transport budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VGF significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on offline RL benchmarks (D4RL, OGBench) and LLM RL tasks. Code and runs can be found at https://ryanxhr.github.io/vgf.

</details>


### [140] [Heat and Matérn Kernels on Matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14331)
*Dmitry Eremeev,Salem Said,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个几何核框架，用于匹配空间，包含平稳核表征、热核与Matérn核扩展、高效评估算法，并探索向树空间的迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于匹配具有离散、非欧几里得的特性，将核方法应用于匹配具有挑战性。需要构建尊重匹配空间自然几何结构的几何核框架

Method: 1. 完全表征平稳核（尊重空间固有对称性）；2. 聚焦热核和Matérn核家族，在平稳性基础上加入平滑性归纳偏置；3. 提出基于zonal多项式的新颖次指数算法进行高效核评估；4. 探索框架向树空间的迁移

Result: 成功将广泛使用的欧几里得核扩展到匹配空间，但朴素评估计算成本超指数级；提出的zonal多项式算法实现次指数计算复杂度；在向树空间迁移方面建立了新的负面结果，识别出重要开放问题

Conclusion: 提出了一个原则性的几何核框架来处理匹配空间，解决了核评估的计算瓶颈，并探索了向树空间的扩展，为生物信息学等领域的应用奠定了基础

Abstract: Applying kernel methods to matchings is challenging due to their discrete, non-Euclidean nature. In this paper, we develop a principled framework for constructing geometric kernels that respect the natural geometry of the space of matchings. To this end, we first provide a complete characterization of stationary kernels, i.e. kernels that respect the inherent symmetries of this space. Because the class of stationary kernels is too broad, we specifically focus on the heat and Matérn kernel families, adding an appropriate inductive bias of smoothness to stationarity. While these families successfully extend widely popular Euclidean kernels to matchings, evaluating them naively incurs a prohibitive super-exponential computational cost. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce and analyze a novel, sub-exponential algorithm leveraging zonal polynomials for efficient kernel evaluation. Finally, motivated by the known bijective correspondence between matchings and phylogenetic trees-a crucial data modality in biology-we explore whether our framework can be seamlessly transferred to the space of trees, establishing novel negative results and identifying a significant open problem.

</details>


### [141] [When Missing Becomes Structure: Intent-Preserving Policy Completion from Financial KOL Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14333)
*Yuncong Liu,Yuan Wan,Zhou Jiang,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KICL框架，通过离线强化学习将社交媒体KOL的投资意图转化为可执行的交易策略，填补KOL陈述中的执行决策空白


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体KOL的投资建议被广泛用作投资指导，但将其转化为可执行的交易策略而不引入未指定的执行决策假设仍是一个开放性问题。KOL陈述存在结构化分离：表达方向意图（买/卖什么及原因）而系统性地未指定执行决策（何时、多少、多久）

Method: 提出意图保持策略补全框架，将KOL话语视为部分交易策略，使用离线强化学习围绕KOL表达的意图补全缺失的执行决策。在YouTube和X平台的多模态KOL话语数据集（2022-2025）上进行实验

Result: KICL在两个平台上均实现最佳回报和夏普比率，同时保持零无支持入场和零方向反转。消融实验显示完整框架相比KOL对齐基线带来18.9%的回报改进

Conclusion: KOL陈述中的执行决策空白不是随机缺陷而是结构化分离，通过意图保持策略补全框架可以有效填补这些空白，将KOL投资意图转化为可执行的交易策略

Abstract: Key Opinion Leader (KOL) discourse on social media is widely consumed as investment guidance, yet turning it into executable trading strategies without injecting assumptions about unspecified execution decisions remains an open problem. We observe that the gaps in KOL statements are not random deficiencies but a structured separation: KOLs express directional intent (what to buy or sell and why) while leaving execution decisions (when, how much, how long) systematically unspecified. Building on this observation, we propose an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL discourse as a partial trading policy and uses offline reinforcement learning to complete the missing execution decisions around the KOL-expressed intent. Experiments on multimodal KOL discourse from YouTube and X (2022-2025) show that KICL achieves the best return and Sharpe ratio on both platforms while maintaining zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablations confirm that the full framework yields an 18.9% return improvement over the KOL-aligned baseline.

</details>


### [142] [Path-Sampled Integrated Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14338)
*Firuz Kamalov,Fadi Thabtah,R. Sivaraj,Neda Abdelhamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出路径采样积分梯度(PS-IG)框架，通过沿线性插值路径采样基线计算期望值来泛化特征归因，证明其与路径加权积分梯度数学等价，将误差收敛率从O(m^{-1/2})提升到O(m^{-1})，并作为方差减少滤波器降低归因方差1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法存在计算效率低、误差收敛慢的问题，需要一种既能保持理论性质又能提高计算效率和准确性的归因框架。

Method: 提出路径采样积分梯度(PS-IG)框架：1) 沿线性插值路径采样基线计算期望值；2) 证明PS-IG与路径加权积分梯度数学等价，要求权重函数匹配采样密度的累积分布函数；3) 通过确定性黎曼和计算随机期望，提高误差收敛率；4) 分析PS-IG作为方差减少滤波器的作用。

Result: 1) PS-IG与路径加权积分梯度数学等价；2) 误差收敛率从O(m^{-1/2})提升到O(m^{-1})；3) 在均匀采样下严格降低归因方差1/3；4) 保持线性、实现不变性等关键公理性质。

Conclusion: PS-IG框架通过路径采样方法有效泛化了特征归因，在保持理论性质的同时显著提高了计算效率和准确性，为特征归因提供了更优的数学基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce path-sampled integrated gradients (PS-IG), a framework that generalizes feature attribution by computing the expected value over baselines sampled along the linear interpolation path. We prove that PS-IG is mathematically equivalent to path-weighted integrated gradients, provided the weighting function matches the cumulative distribution function of the sampling density. This equivalence allows the stochastic expectation to be evaluated via a deterministic Riemann sum, improving the error convergence rate from $O(m^{-1/2})$ to $O(m^{-1})$ for smooth models. Furthermore, we demonstrate analytically that PS-IG functions as a variance-reducing filter against gradient noise - strictly lowering attribution variance by a factor of 1/3 under uniform sampling - while preserving key axiomatic properties such as linearity and implementation invariance.

</details>


### [143] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Best-Arm Identification Under Bounded Systematic Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14345)
*Tianhao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在存在系统性偏差的情况下，为自主推理和具身规划中的节点扩展提供安全保证的方法，通过将节点扩展过程建模为带偏差的局部最优臂识别问题，并推导出样本复杂度和安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主推理和具身规划中搜索深度的增加，候选动作空间呈指数级扩展，严重消耗计算资源。虽然启发式剪枝是常见的对策，但当替代模型（如LLMs）表现出系统性评估偏差时，它缺乏正式的安全保证。因此需要一种在存在系统性偏差的情况下，为节点扩展提供安全保证的方法。

Method: 将节点扩展过程建模为动态边界上的局部最优臂识别问题，假设存在有界系统性偏差L。通过反演Lambert W函数，建立了加法样本复杂度O((Δ-4L)^{-2})，表明只有当经验奖励差距超过4L时，安全节点消除才可行。同时提供了信息论下界Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})来确认偏差搜索的结构限制。

Result: 在合成树和复杂推理任务上的评估表明，遵守这种局部安全边界能够成功保留最优轨迹，同时最大化样本分配效率。当经验奖励差距超过4L时，可以实现安全节点消除，而低于此阈值则无法保证安全性。

Conclusion: 该研究为在存在系统性偏差的搜索过程中提供安全保证提供了理论框架，通过建立明确的样本复杂度界限和安全边界，使得在自主推理和规划中能够更可靠地进行节点扩展和剪枝决策。

Abstract: As search depth increases in autonomous reasoning and embodied planning, the candidate action space expands exponentially, heavily taxing computational budgets. While heuristic pruning is a common countermeasure, it operates without formal safety guarantees when surrogate models (like LLMs) exhibit systematic evaluation biases. This paper frames the node expansion process as a localized Best-Arm Identification (BAI) problem over dynamic frontiers, subject to a bounded systematic bias $L$. By inverting the Lambert W function, we establish an additive sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}((Δ-4L)^{-2})$, which indicates that safe node elimination is only feasible when the empirical reward gap exceeds $4L$. We complement this with an information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})$ to confirm the structural limits of biased search. Subsequent evaluations on both synthetic trees and complex reasoning tasks demonstrate that adhering to this local safety boundary successfully preserves optimal trajectories while maximizing sample allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [144] [Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment with Multiple Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14379)
*Qi Zhang,Dawei Wang,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MSDDA是一个无需重新训练的多目标扩散模型对齐框架，通过步级RL公式化获得闭式解的最优反向去噪分布，避免了近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好本质上是多元的，扩散模型需要平衡多个下游目标（如美学质量和图文一致性）。现有多目标方法要么依赖昂贵的多目标RL微调，要么在去噪时融合单独对齐的模型，通常需要奖励值（或其梯度）访问权限，并引入近似误差。

Method: 提出步级RL公式化解决最优策略识别难题，在此基础上提出MSDDA框架：通过闭式解获得最优反向去噪分布，其均值和方差直接由单目标基础模型表达，无需重新训练。

Result: 证明该去噪时目标与步级RL微调完全等价，不引入近似误差。数值结果表明该方法优于现有的去噪时方法。

Conclusion: MSDDA为扩散模型的多目标对齐提供了一个理论严谨且实用的框架，避免了重新训练成本，实现了精确的多目标平衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful tool for aligning diffusion models with human preferences, typically by optimizing a single reward function under a KL regularization constraint. In practice, however, human preferences are inherently pluralistic, and aligned models must balance multiple downstream objectives, such as aesthetic quality and text-image consistency. Existing multi-objective approaches either rely on costly multi-objective RL fine-tuning or on fusing separately aligned models at denoising time, but they generally require access to reward values (or their gradients) and/or introduce approximation error in the resulting denoising objectives. In this paper, we revisit the problem of RL fine-tuning for diffusion models and address the intractability of identifying the optimal policy by introducing a step-level RL formulation. Building on this, we further propose Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA), a retraining-free framework for aligning diffusion models with multiple objectives, obtaining the optimal reverse denoising distribution in closed form, with mean and variance expressed directly in terms of single-objective base models. We prove that this denoising-time objective is exactly equivalent to the step-level RL fine-tuning, introducing no approximation error. Moreover, we provide numerical results, which indicate our method outperforms existing denoising-time approaches.

</details>


### [145] [Auxiliary Finite-Difference Residual-Gradient Regularization for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14472)
*Stavros Kassinos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合PINN设计：PDE残差仍基于自动微分，而有限差分仅出现在弱辅助项中，用于惩罚采样残差场的梯度。该方法通过FD项正则化残差场而不替换PDE残差本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN通常使用单一标量损失函数，即使所关注的物理量更为具体。研究旨在开发一种混合设计，在保持PDE残差自动微分计算的同时，通过有限差分正则化残差场，以更针对性地提升特定物理量的预测精度。

Method: 采用两阶段研究：阶段1为受控泊松基准测试，比较基线PINN、FD残差梯度正则器和匹配的AD残差梯度基线；阶段2将相同逻辑应用于三维环形热传导基准测试（PINN3D），在波浪形外壁附近实现贴体壳层网格的辅助正则化。

Result: 阶段1中，FD正则器重现了残差梯度控制的主要效果，同时揭示了场精度与残差清洁度之间的权衡。阶段2中，壳层正则器改善了应用相关的物理量（外壁通量和边界条件行为）。在Kourkoutas-beta优化器下，固定壳层权重5e-4配置最可靠：相比无壳层项，外壁BC RMSE从1.22e-2降至9.29e-4，壁通量RMSE从9.21e-3降至9.63e-4。

Conclusion: 研究支持混合PINN的针对性观点：当辅助FD正则器与所关注的物理量（如外壁通量）对齐时最为有效。该方法通过有限差分正则化残差场而不替换PDE残差本身，为特定物理量的精度提升提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are often selected by a single scalar loss even when the quantity of interest is more specific. We study a hybrid design in which the governing PDE residual remains automatic-differentiation (AD) based, while finite differences (FD) appear only in a weak auxiliary term that penalizes gradients of the sampled residual field. The FD term regularizes the residual field without replacing the PDE residual itself.
  We examine this idea in two stages. Stage 1 is a controlled Poisson benchmark comparing a baseline PINN, the FD residual-gradient regularizer, and a matched AD residual-gradient baseline. Stage 2 transfers the same logic to a three-dimensional annular heat-conduction benchmark (PINN3D), where baseline errors concentrate near a wavy outer wall and the auxiliary grid is implemented as a body-fitted shell adjacent to the wall.
  In Stage 1, the FD regularizer reproduces the main effect of residual-gradient control while exposing a trade-off between field accuracy and residual cleanliness. In Stage 2, the shell regularizer improves the application-facing quantities, namely outer-wall flux and boundary-condition behavior. Across seeds 0-5 and 100k epochs, the most reliable tested configuration is a fixed shell weight of 5e-4 under the Kourkoutas-beta optimizer regime: relative to a matched run without the shell term, it reduces the mean outer-wall BC RMSE from 1.22e-2 to 9.29e-4 and the mean wall-flux RMSE from 9.21e-3 to 9.63e-4. Adam with beta2=0.999 becomes usable when the initial learning rate is reduced to 1e-3, although its shell benefit is less robust than under Kourkoutas-beta. Overall, the results support a targeted view of hybrid PINNs: an auxiliary-only FD regularizer is most valuable when it is aligned with the physical quantity of interest, here the outer-wall flux.

</details>


### [146] [Scouting By Reward: VLM-TO-IRL-Driven Player Selection For Esports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14474)
*Qing Yan,Wenyu Yang,Yufei Wang,Wenhao Ma,Linchong Hu,Yifei Jin,Anton Dahbura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于逆强化学习的电竞选手选拔框架，通过从职业选手游戏演示中学习奖励函数，根据与目标明星选手的风格匹配度来排名候选人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电竞选拔依赖人工视频审查和聚合性能指标，无法捕捉决定选手是否适合特定战术原型的细微决策模式，需要更精准的风格评估方法。

Method: 采用逆强化学习框架，构建多模态双分支架构：一支编码高分辨率游戏遥测的结构化状态-动作轨迹，另一支编码视觉语言模型从比赛录像生成的时序对齐战术伪解说。通过生成对抗模仿学习目标融合表示，判别器学习精英选手的独特机械和战术特征。

Result: 该框架从通用技能评估转向基于奖励的选拔，提供可扩展的工作流感知数字孪生系统，支持数据驱动的阵容构建和大规模候选人池的定向人才发现。

Conclusion: 通过将风格化选手评估重新定义为逆强化学习问题，提出的框架能够学习职业选手特定的奖励函数，实现基于与目标明星选手风格匹配度的候选人排名，为电竞组织提供更精准的选拔工具。

Abstract: Traditional esports scouting workflows rely heavily on manual video review and aggregate performance metrics, which often fail to capture the nuanced decision-making patterns necessary to determine if a prospect fits a specific tactical archetype. To address this, we reframe style-based player evaluation in esports as an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) problem. In this paper, we introduce a novel player selection framework that learns professional-specific reward functions from logged gameplay demonstrations, allowing organizations to rank candidates by their stylistic alignment with a target star player. Our proposed architecture utilizes a multimodal, two-branch intake: one branch encodes structured state-action trajectories derived from high-resolution in-game telemetry, while the second encodes temporally aligned tactical pseudo-commentary generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) from broadcast footage. These representations are fused and evaluated via a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) objective, where a discriminator learns to capture the unique mechanical and tactical signatures of elite professionals. By transitioning from generic skill estimation to scouting "by reward," this framework provides a scalable, workflow-aware digital twin system that enables data-driven roster construction and targeted talent discovery across massive candidate pools.

</details>


### [147] [CI-CBM: Class-Incremental Concept Bottleneck Model for Interpretable Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14519)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tara Javidi,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CI-CBM是一种用于类增量学习的可解释概念瓶颈模型，通过概念正则化和伪概念生成技术，在保持模型可解释性的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题严重，现有方法往往需要在模型可解释性和准确性之间做出妥协。作者希望开发一种既能保持可解释决策过程，又能维持高性能的类增量学习方法。

Method: 提出ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM)，采用概念正则化和伪概念生成技术。概念正则化帮助模型在增量学习阶段保持先前学到的概念知识，伪概念生成则用于处理新任务中未定义的概念。模型支持预训练和从头训练两种场景。

Result: 在七个数据集上的评估显示，CI-CBM达到了与黑盒模型相当的性能，同时比之前的可解释方法平均提升了36%的准确率。模型不仅能提供单个输入的可解释决策，还能展示全局可理解的决策规则。

Conclusion: CI-CBM证明了在增量学习过程中可以保持人类可理解的概念，同时不损害模型性能。该方法在预训练和非预训练场景下都有效，为可解释的类增量学习提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning, in which models often forget previous knowledge when fine-tuned on a new task. This issue is especially pronounced in class incremental learning (CIL), which is the most challenging setting in continual learning. Existing methods to address catastrophic forgetting often sacrifice either model interpretability or accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM), which leverage effective techniques, including concept regularization and pseudo-concept generation to maintain interpretable decision processes throughout incremental learning phases. Through extensive evaluation on seven datasets, CI-CBM achieves comparable performance to black-box models and outperforms previous interpretable approaches in CIL, with an average 36% accuracy gain. CICBM provides interpretable decisions on individual inputs and understandable global decision rules, as shown in our experiments, thereby demonstrating that human understandable concepts can be maintained during incremental learning without compromising model performance. Our approach is effective in both pretrained and non-pretrained scenarios; in the latter, the backbone is trained from scratch during the first learning phase. Code is publicly available at github.com/importAmir/CI-CBM.

</details>


### [148] [CSRA: Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation for Robust Sepsis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14532)
*Honglin Guo,Rihao Chang,He Jiao,Weizhi Nie,Zhongheng Zhang,Yuehao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSRA是一种用于短窗口ICU时间序列预测的受控谱残差增强框架，通过系统级表示和谱域扰动生成临床合理的轨迹变化，提升脓毒症风险预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症早期预警需要准确的短期风险预测，但短观察窗口提供有限历史证据，长预测时域减少有效监督样本，现有方法面临数据不足的挑战。

Method: CSRA框架首先按临床系统分组变量，提取系统级和全局表示；然后在谱域进行输入自适应的残差扰动，生成结构化且临床合理的轨迹变化；通过端到端训练结合锚点一致性损失和控制器正则化提升增强稳定性和可控性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV脓毒症队列实验中，CSRA相比无增强基线在回归任务上MSE降低10.2%，MAE降低3.7%，分类任务也有持续提升；在更短观察窗口、更长预测时域和更小训练数据规模下保持更优性能，在外部数据集(ZiGongICUinfection)上同样有效。

Conclusion: CSRA通过受控谱残差增强有效解决了短窗口ICU时间序列预测的数据不足问题，在临床约束条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为脓毒症早期预警提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of future risk and disease progression in sepsis is clinically important for early warning and timely intervention in intensive care. However, short-window sepsis prediction remains challenging, because shorter observation windows provide limited historical evidence, whereas longer prediction horizons reduce the number of patient trajectories with valid future supervision. To address this problem, we propose CSRA, a Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation framework for short-window multi-system ICU time series. CSRA first groups variables by clinical systems and extracts system-level and global representations. It then performs input-adaptive residual perturbation in the spectral domain to generate structured and clinically plausible trajectory variations. To improve augmentation stability and controllability, CSRA is trained end-to-end with the downstream predictor under a unified objective, together with anchor consistency loss and controller regularization. Experiments on a MIMIC-IV sepsis cohort across multiple downstream models show that CSRA is consistently competitive and often superior, reducing regression error by 10.2\% in MSE and 3.7\% in MAE over the non-augmentation baseline, while also yielding consistent gains on classification. CSRA further maintains more favorable performance under shorter observation windows, longer prediction horizons, and smaller training data scales, while also remaining effective on an external clinical dataset~(ZiGongICUinfection), indicating stronger robustness and generalizability in clinically constrained settings.

</details>


### [149] [An unsupervised decision-support framework for multivariate biomarker analysis in athlete monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14534)
*Fernando Barcelos Rosito,Sebastião De Jesus Menezes,Simone Ferreira Sturza,Adriana Seixas,Muriel Figueredo Franco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督多变量框架，从运动员生物标志物数据中识别潜在生理状态，无需损伤标签，可区分机械损伤与代谢应激，发现传统监测遗漏的沉默风险表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测面临小样本、异质生物标志物尺度、重复采样可行性有限、缺乏可靠损伤真实标签等限制，这些限制降低了传统单变量和二元风险模型的解释性和实用性。

Method: 提出模块化计算框架，在联合生物标志物空间中操作，整合预处理、临床安全筛查、无监督聚类和基于质心的生理解释。仅使用业余足球运动员在竞争性微周期中的数据学习特征。通过合成数据增强评估鲁棒性和可扩展性。使用Ward层次聚类支持监测和病因学区分，高斯混合模型用于高维设置中的结构稳定性分析。

Result: 框架识别出能区分机械损伤与代谢应激同时保持稳态状态的连贯特征。合成数据增强证明了可行性，并检测到通常被单变量监测遗漏的潜在沉默风险表型。结构分析表明在增强和高维设置下具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从多变量生物标志物数据中无需损伤标签即可解释性地识别潜在生理状态。通过区分机制并揭示传统监测未捕获的沉默风险模式，为个体化运动员监测和决策制定提供可操作的见解。

Abstract: Purpose. Athlete monitoring is constrained by small cohorts, heterogeneous biomarker scales, limited feasibility of repeated sampling, and the lack of reliable injury ground truth. These limitations reduce the interpretability and utility of traditional univariate and binary risk models. This study addresses these challenges by proposing an unsupervised multivariate framework to identify latent physiological states in athletes using real data. Methods. We propose a modular computational framework that operates in the joint biomarker space, integrating preprocessing, clinical safety screening, unsupervised clustering, and centroid-based physiological interpretation. Profiles are learned exclusively from amateur soccer players during a competitive microcycle. Synthetic data augmentation evaluates robustness and scalability. Ward hierarchical clustering supports monitoring and etiological differentiation, while Gaussian Mixture Models (GMM) enable structural stability analysis in high-dimensional settings. Results. The framework identifies coherent profiles that distinguish mechanical damage from metabolic stress while preserving homeostatic states. Synthetic data augmentation demonstrates feasibility and detection of latent silent risk phenotypes typically missed by univariate monitoring. Structural analyses indicate robustness under augmentation and higher-dimensional settings. Conclusion. The framework enables interpretable identification of latent physiological states from multivariate biomarker data without injury labels. By distinguishing mechanisms and revealing silent risk patterns not captured by conventional monitoring, it provides actionable insights for individualized athlete monitoring and decision making.

</details>


### [150] [Predicting Post-Traumatic Epilepsy from Clinical Records using Large Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14547)
*Wenhui Cui,Nicholas Swingle,Anand A. Joshi,Dileep Nair,Richard M. Leahy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用预训练大语言模型编码临床记录，结合梯度提升树分类器预测创伤后癫痫，相比传统表格特征性能显著提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创伤后癫痫（PTE）是创伤性脑损伤后的严重神经系统并发症，早期预测面临临床数据异质性、阳性病例有限以及依赖资源密集型神经影像数据的挑战。研究探索是否仅使用常规收集的急性期临床记录就能支持早期PTE预测。

Method: 使用TRACK-TBI队列的精选子集，开发自动化PTE预测框架：1）使用预训练大语言模型作为固定特征提取器编码临床记录；2）评估表格特征、LLM生成的嵌入表示以及混合特征表示；3）在分层交叉验证下使用梯度提升树分类器进行评估。

Result: LLM嵌入通过捕捉上下文临床信息相比单独使用表格特征实现了性能提升。最佳性能通过结合表格特征和LLM嵌入的模态感知特征融合策略实现，AUC-ROC为0.892，AUPRC为0.798。急性创伤后癫痫发作、损伤严重程度、神经外科干预和ICU住院是关键预测因素。

Conclusion: 常规急性期临床记录包含适合早期PTE风险预测的信息，使用LLM嵌入结合梯度提升树分类器的方法代表了一种有前景的影像学预测补充方案。

Abstract: Objective: Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) is a debilitating neurological disorder that develops after traumatic brain injury (TBI). Early prediction of PTE remains challenging due to heterogeneous clinical data, limited positive cases, and reliance on resource-intensive neuroimaging data. We investigate whether routinely collected acute clinical records alone can support early PTE prediction using language model-based approaches. Methods: Using a curated subset of the TRACK-TBI cohort, we developed an automated PTE prediction framework that implements pretrained large language models (LLMs) as fixed feature extractors to encode clinical records. Tabular features, LLM-generated embeddings, and hybrid feature representations were evaluated using gradient-boosted tree classifiers under stratified cross-validation. Results: LLM embeddings achieved performance improvements by capturing contextual clinical information compared to using tabular features alone. The best performance was achieved by a modality-aware feature fusion strategy combining tabular features and LLM embeddings, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.892 and AUPRC of 0.798. Acute post-traumatic seizures, injury severity, neurosurgical intervention, and ICU stay are key contributors to the predictive performance. Significance: These findings demonstrate that routine acute clinical records contain information suitable for early PTE risk prediction using LLM embeddings in conjunction with gradient-boosted tree classifiers. This approach represents a promising complement to imaging-based prediction.

</details>


### [151] [CLion: Efficient Cautious Lion Optimizer with Enhanced Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14587)
*Feihu Huang,Guanyi Zhang,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lion优化器的泛化性能，提出了具有更低泛化误差的CLion优化器，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Lion优化器在机器学习中表现出色且其收敛性已被研究，但其泛化分析仍然缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究Lion优化器的泛化特性，并设计具有更好泛化性能的新优化器。

Method: 基于数学归纳法的算法稳定性分析Lion优化器的泛化误差。设计新颖的CLion优化器，通过谨慎使用符号函数来增强泛化能力。使用算法稳定性理论分析泛化误差，并证明CLion在非凸随机优化下的收敛性。

Result: 证明Lion优化器具有O(1/(Nτ^T))的泛化误差，其中τ是梯度估计器中非零元素的最小绝对值。发现SignSGD具有与Lion相同的泛化误差。提出的CLion优化器具有更低的O(1/N)泛化误差，并在非凸随机优化下具有O(√d/T^{1/4})的收敛速率。实验验证了CLion的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次对Lion优化器进行了泛化分析，证明了其泛化误差界限。提出的CLion优化器在保持良好收敛性的同时显著改善了泛化性能，为基于符号的优化算法提供了理论保证和实用改进。

Abstract: Lion optimizer is a popular learning-based optimization algorithm in machine learning, which shows impressive performance in training many deep learning models. Although convergence property of the Lion optimizer has been studied, its generalization analysis is still missing. To fill this gap, we study generalization property of the Lion via algorithmic stability based on the mathematical induction. Specifically, we prove that the Lion has a generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $τ>0$ denotes the smallest absolute value of non-zero element in gradient estimator, and $T$ is the total iteration number. In addition, we obtain an interesting byproduct that the SignSGD algorithm has the same generalization error as the Lion. To enhance generalization of the Lion, we design a novel efficient Cautious Lion (i.e., CLion) optimizer by cautiously using sign function.
