Moreover, we prove that our CLion has a lower generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{N})$ than $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$ of the Lion, since the parameter $τ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we study convergence property of our CLion optimizer, and prove that our CLion has a fast convergence rate of $O(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{T^{1/4}})$ under $\ell_1$-norm of gradient for nonconvex stochastic optimization, where $d$ denotes the model dimension.
  Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate effectiveness of our CLion optimizer.

</details>


### [152] [ConfLayers: Adaptive Confidence-based Layer Skipping for Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14612)
*Walaa Amer,Uday das,Fadi Kurdahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConfLayers提出了一种基于置信度的动态层跳过方法，用于自推测解码中的草稿模型构建，通过自适应阈值选择跳过层，无需训练层跳过策略，实现1.4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自推测解码通过使用紧凑版模型作为草稿模型来加速大语言模型推理，现有方法通常通过动态学习跳过哪些层来构建草稿模型，但启发式方法可能更简单有效。本文旨在开发一种无需训练、基于置信度的动态层跳过方法。

Method: 提出ConfLayers框架：迭代计算所有层的置信度分数，基于自适应阈值选择要跳过的层，评估结果集的性能，直到没有进一步改进或达到最大迭代次数。这是一种即插即用的动态方法，避免训练层跳过策略的复杂性。

Result: 在不同模型和数据集上的性能评估表明，ConfLayers相比原始LLM生成提供高达1.4倍的加速，同时保持输出质量，提供更一致的速度-质量权衡，并保持草稿模型对不同任务和数据集的适应性。

Conclusion: ConfLayers是一种有效的自推测解码草稿模型构建方法，通过基于置信度的动态层跳过实现加速，避免了训练开销，在保持质量的同时提供显著的速度提升。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding is an inference technique for large language models designed to speed up generation without sacrificing output quality. It combines fast, approximate decoding using a compact version of the model as a draft model with selective re-evaluation by the full target model. Some existing methods form the draft model by dynamically learning which layers to skip during inference, effectively creating a smaller subnetwork to speed up computation. However, using heuristic-based approaches to select layers to skip can often be simpler and more effective. In this paper, we propose ConfLayers, a dynamic plug-and-play approach to forming the draft model in self-speculative decoding via confidence-based intermediate layer skipping. The process iteratively computes confidence scores for all layers, selects layers to skip based on an adaptive threshold, evaluates the performance of the resulting set, and updates the best selection until no further improvement is achieved or a maximum number of iterations is reached. This framework avoids the overhead and complexity of training a layer skipping policy and can provide more consistent speed-quality trade-offs while preserving the adaptivity of the draft model to diverse tasks and datasets. The performance evaluation of ConfLayers across different models and datasets shows that our novel approach offers up to 1.4x speedup over vanilla LLM generation.

</details>


### [153] [ELMoE-3D: Leveraging Intrinsic Elasticity of MoE for Hybrid-Bonding-Enabled Self-Speculative Decoding in On-Premises Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14626)
*Yuseon Choi,Jingu Lee,Jungjun Oh,Sunjoo Whang,Byeongcheol Kim,Minsung Kim,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELMoE-3D：基于混合键合技术的软硬件协同设计框架，通过弹性自推测解码和位切片架构，在3D堆叠硬件上实现MoE模型的高效推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型已成为大规模语言模型的主流架构，但在本地部署时面临内存瓶颈问题。传统内存中心架构在MoE低算术强度下计算利用率不足，推测解码在MoE中因需要加载专家模型而效果受限，尤其在低批次大小下。

Method: 提出ELMoE-3D框架，结合缓存加速和推测解码。引入弹性自推测解码，利用MoE的两个内在弹性轴（专家轴和位轴）构建专家缓存和强对齐自草稿模型。采用LSB增强的位切片架构，利用位切片表示中的固有冗余原生支持位嵌套执行。

Result: 在3D堆叠硬件上，ELMoE-3D在批次大小1-16范围内相比朴素MoE服务平均获得6.6倍加速和4.4倍能效提升，相比最佳先前加速器基线获得2.2倍加速和1.4倍能效提升。

Conclusion: ELMoE-3D通过软硬件协同设计，有效解决了MoE模型推理中的内存瓶颈问题，实现了跨批次大小的整体加速，为大规模MoE模型的高效部署提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the dominant architecture for large-scale language models, yet on-premises serving remains fundamentally memory-bound as batching turns sparse per-token compute into dense memory activation. Memory-centric architectures (PIM, NMP) improve bandwidth but leave compute underutilized under MoE's low arithmetic intensity at high batch sizes. Speculative decoding (SD) trades idle compute for fewer target invocations, yet verification must load experts even for rejected tokens, severely limiting its benefit in MoE especially at low batch sizes. We propose ELMoE-3D, a hybrid-bonding (HB)-based HW-SW co-designed framework that unifies cache-based acceleration and speculative decoding to offer overall speedup across batch sizes. We identify two intrinsic elasticity axes of MoE-expert and bit-and jointly scale them to construct Elastic Self-Speculative Decoding (Elastic-SD), which serves as both an expert cache and a strongly aligned self-draft model accelerated by high HB bandwidth. Our LSB-augmented bit-sliced architecture exploits inherent redundancy in bit-slice representations to natively support bit-nested execution. On our 3D-stacked hardware, ELMoE-3D achieves an average $6.6\times$ speedup and $4.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over naive MoE serving on xPU across batch sizes 1-16, and delivers $2.2\times$ speedup and $1.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over the best-performing prior accelerator baseline.

</details>


### [154] [Mean Flow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14698)
*Xiaoyi Dong,Xi Sheryl Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MeanFlow模型作为扩散模型的替代，用于强化学习策略表示，显著提升训练和推理效率，同时保持或超越扩散模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在在线强化学习中作为策略表示表现出色，但其迭代生成过程导致训练和推理开销过大。需要一种既能保持表达能力又能提高效率的替代方案。

Method: 提出MeanFlow模型（少步流式生成模型）作为策略表示，在最大熵强化学习框架下通过软策略迭代优化MeanFlow策略，解决了动作似然评估和软策略改进两个关键挑战。

Result: 在MuJoCo和DeepMind Control Suite基准测试中，Mean Flow Policy Optimization (MFPO)方法达到或超过当前扩散基线的性能，同时显著减少训练和推理时间。

Conclusion: MeanFlow模型是扩散模型的有效替代方案，能够在保持强化学习性能的同时大幅提升效率，为实际应用提供了更实用的策略表示方法。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive policy representations for online reinforcement learning (RL). However, their iterative generative processes introduce substantial training and inference overhead. To overcome this limitation, we propose to represent policies using MeanFlow models, a class of few-step flow-based generative models, to improve training and inference efficiency over diffusion-based RL approaches. To promote exploration, we optimize MeanFlow policies under the maximum entropy RL framework via soft policy iteration, and address two key challenges specific to MeanFlow policies: action likelihood evaluation and soft policy improvement. Experiments on MuJoCo and DeepMind Control Suite benchmarks demonstrate that our method, Mean Flow Policy Optimization (MFPO), achieves performance comparable to or exceeding current diffusion-based baselines while considerably reducing training and inference time. Our code is available at https://github.com/MFPolicy/MFPO.

</details>


### [155] [Gating Enables Curvature: A Geometric Expressivity Gap in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14702)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过几何视角分析注意力机制，发现无门控注意力受限于平坦统计流形，而乘性门控能实现非平坦几何（包括正曲率流形），这解释了门控注意力在非线性任务中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管门控注意力在大型语言模型中成功应用并提升了性能和训练稳定性，但其数学原理和几何含义仍不明确。研究者希望通过几何分析理解门控与非门控注意力的本质差异。

Method: 将注意力输出建模为高斯分布的均值参数，分析其诱导的Fisher-Rao几何。通过统计流形理论，比较无门控和乘性门控注意力的几何特性。

Result: 无门控注意力受限于内在平坦的统计流形（仿射结构限制），而乘性门控能实现非平坦几何，包括正曲率流形。门控模型在非线性决策边界任务中表现更好，在线性任务中无一致优势。还发现了曲率在组合下积累的结构化机制。

Conclusion: 乘性门控通过引入非线性几何表达能力，弥补了无门控注意力的几何表达性差距，这解释了门控注意力在复杂任务中的性能优势，并为深度模型设计提供了几何理论依据。

Abstract: Multiplicative gating is widely used in neural architectures and has recently been applied to attention layers to improve performance and training stability in large language models. Despite the success of gated attention, the mathematical implications of gated attention mechanisms remain poorly understood. We study attention through the geometry of its representations by modeling outputs as mean parameters of Gaussian distributions and analyzing the induced Fisher--Rao geometry. We show that ungated attention operator is restricted to intrinsically flat statistical manifolds due to its affine structure, while multiplicative gating enables non-flat geometries, including positively curved manifolds that are unattainable in the ungated setting. These results establish a geometric expressivity gap between ungated and gated attention. Empirically, we show that gated models exhibit higher representation curvature and improved performance on tasks requiring nonlinear decision boundaries whereas they provide no consistent advantage on tasks with linear decision boundaries. Furthermore, we identify a structured regime in which curvature accumulates under composition, yielding a systematic depth amplification effect.

</details>


### [156] [A Mechanistic Account of Attention Sinks in GPT-2: One Circuit, Broader Implications for Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14722)
*Yuval Ran-Milo,Hila Ofek,Shahar Mendel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer模型中的注意力下沉现象（attention sink）是由学习到的查询偏置、第一层MLP对位置编码的变换以及键投影结构三者共同作用导致的，且每个组件单独移除后模型仍会出现注意力下沉，表明不同架构可能通过不同电路产生此现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型普遍表现出注意力下沉现象：对第一个位置给予不成比例的高注意力。本研究旨在深入理解GPT-2风格模型中这一行为的成因机制，为缓解注意力下沉现象提供理论基础，并推动更广泛的关于注意力下沉为何出现的探究。

Method: 采用结构分析与因果干预相结合的方法，在GPT-2风格模型（具有学习到的查询偏置和绝对位置嵌入）上进行研究。研究验证了自然语言、数学和代码三种不同类型的输入数据，通过识别关键组件并测试其可移除性来探究注意力下沉的成因。

Result: 发现注意力下沉源于三个组件的相互作用：(1)学习到的查询偏置，(2)第一层MLP对位置编码的变换，(3)键投影的结构。关键发现是每个组件单独都是可移除的：即使省略其中任何一个组件，架构仍会稳健地表现出注意力下沉现象。

Conclusion: 注意力下沉可能通过不同架构中的不同电路产生，这一发现为缓解注意力下沉提供了信息，并激励了对注意力下沉为何出现的更广泛研究。研究结果表明注意力下沉现象具有多种可能的成因机制，而非单一固定模式。

Abstract: Transformers commonly exhibit an attention sink: disproportionately high attention to the first position. We study this behavior in GPT-2-style models with learned query biases and absolute positional embeddings. Combining structural analysis with causal interventions, validated across natural-language, mathematical, and code inputs, we find that the sink arises from the interaction among (i) a learned query bias, (ii) the first-layer MLP transformation of the positional encoding, and (iii) structure in the key projection. Crucially, each component we identify is individually dispensable: architectures omitting each of them robustly exhibit sinks. This indicates that attention sinks may arise through distinct circuits across architectures. These findings inform mitigation of sinks, and motivate broader investigation into why sinks emerge.

</details>


### [157] [Constraint-based Pre-training: From Structured Constraints to Scalable Model Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14769)
*Fu Feng,Yucheng Xie,Ruixiao Shi,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeiT提出基于约束的预训练范式，通过结构化约束将尺寸无关知识解耦到可重用权重模板中，将尺寸特定适应分配给轻量级权重缩放器，实现可变尺寸模型初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练产生固定尺寸模型，而实际部署需要不同尺寸模型，当目标模型尺寸与预训练尺寸不同时存在局限性。需要解决可变尺寸模型初始化问题。

Method: 提出约束基预训练范式，使用Kronecker基约束正则化预训练过程。模型参数表示为权重模板的组合（拼接和加权聚合），由轻量级权重缩放器控制自适应连接，这些缩放器参数从有限数据中学习。

Result: 在图像分类、图像生成和具身控制等感知和具身学习任务中，WeiT在初始化不同深度和宽度模型方面达到最先进性能。对Transformer和卷积架构都有效，即使在完全训练下也能实现更快收敛和更好性能。

Conclusion: WeiT通过约束基预训练范式成功解决了可变尺寸模型初始化问题，将尺寸无关知识与尺寸特定适应解耦，实现了灵活高效的跨下游尺寸模型权重构建。

Abstract: The pre-training and fine-tuning paradigm has become the dominant approach for model adaptation. However, conventional pre-training typically yields models at a fixed scale, whereas practical deployment often requires models of varying sizes, exposing its limitations when target model scales differ from those used during pre-training. To address this, we propose an innovative constraint-based pre-training paradigm that imposes structured constraints during pre-training to disentangle size-agnostic knowledge into reusable weight templates, while assigning size-specific adaptation to lightweight weight scalers, thereby reformulating variable-sized model initialization as a multi-task adaptation problem. Within this paradigm, we further introduce WeiT, which employs Kronecker-based constraints to regularize the pre-training process. Specifically, model parameters are represented as compositions of weight templates via concatenation and weighted aggregation, with adaptive connections governed by lightweight weight scalers whose parameters are learned from limited data. This design enables flexible and efficient construction of model weights across diverse downstream scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of WeiT, achieving state-of-the-art performance in initializing models with varying depths and widths across a broad range of perception and embodied learning tasks, including Image Classification, Image Generation, and Embodied Control. Moreover, its effectiveness generalizes to both Transformer-based and Convolution-based architectures, consistently enabling faster convergence and improved performance even under full training.

</details>


### [158] [Does RL Expand the Capability Boundary of LLM Agents? A PASS@(k,T) Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14877)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Wenjing Yan,Xiaodan Shao,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RL在工具使用任务中确实扩展了LLM能力边界，而非仅提升可靠性；在静态推理任务中则仅改善效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习是否真正扩展了LLM智能体的能力边界，还是仅使其更可靠。针对工具使用场景，研究RL能否支持静态重采样无法恢复的组合式策略

Method: 引入PASS@(k,T)二维评估指标，分离能力扩展与效率改进；对比RL与基础模型在工具使用任务上的表现；分析监督微调与RL训练数据的匹配效果；进行机制分析探究RL如何改变策略分布

Result: 在组合式、顺序信息收集任务中，RL确实扩展了能力边界：RL智能体的通过率曲线高于基础模型，且差距随k增大而扩大而非收敛；在简单任务中RL表现与先前研究一致；监督微调在同一任务上反而使能力边界退化

Conclusion: RL对LLM的影响因任务类型而异：在工具使用等组合式任务中真正扩展能力边界，在静态推理任务中仅改善效率；自我导向探索是能力扩展的关键因素；RL通过重加权策略分布，提升信息整合能力

Abstract: Does reinforcement learning genuinely expand what LLM agents can do, or merely make them more reliable? For static reasoning, recent work answers the second: base and RL pass@k curves converge at large k. We ask whether this holds for agentic tool use, where T rounds of interaction enable compositional strategies that re-sampling cannot recover. We introduce PASS@(k,T), a two-dimensional metric that jointly varies sampling budget k and interaction depth T, separating capability expansion from efficiency improvement. Our main finding is that, contrary to the static-reasoning result, tool-use RL genuinely enlarges the capability boundary: the RL agent's pass-curve pulls above the base model's and the gap widens at large k rather than converging. The expansion is specific to compositional, sequential information gathering; on simpler tasks RL behaves as prior work predicts. Under matched training data, supervised fine-tuning regresses the boundary on the same compositional tasks, isolating self-directed exploration as the causal factor. Mechanism analysis shows RL reweights the base strategy distribution toward the subset whose downstream reasoning more often yields a correct answer, with the improvement concentrated on how the agent integrates retrieved information. These results reconcile optimistic and pessimistic readings of RL for LLMs: both are correct, on different task types.

</details>


### [159] [xFODE+: Explainable Type-2 Fuzzy Additive ODEs for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14880)
*Ertugrul Kececi,Tufan Kumbasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: xFODE+是一种可解释的Type-2模糊加性ODE模型，用于系统辨识中的不确定性量化，在保持预测精度的同时提供预测区间和物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推动了数据驱动的系统辨识，但可靠使用需要不确定性量化。现有模糊ODE模型能产生预测区间但可解释性有限，需要同时具备准确预测、不确定性量化和可解释性的系统辨识模型。

Method: 提出xFODE+模型，使用区间Type-2模糊逻辑系统实现模糊加性模型，约束隶属函数到两个相邻规则的激活区域以限制重叠并保持局部透明性。通过IT2-FLS产生的类型约简集构建状态更新和预测区间，在深度学习框架中使用联合优化预测精度和预测区间质量的复合损失函数进行训练。

Result: 在基准系统辨识数据集上，xFODE+在预测区间质量上与FODE相当，预测精度可比，同时提供了可解释性。

Conclusion: xFODE+是一种有效的可解释系统辨识模型，能够同时提供准确的点预测、高质量的预测区间和物理有意义的增量状态解释，解决了现有模糊ODE模型可解释性不足的问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in Deep Learning (DL) have boosted data-driven System Identification (SysID), but reliable use requires Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) alongside accurate predictions. Although UQ-capable models such as Fuzzy ODE (FODE) can produce Prediction Intervals (PIs), they offer limited interpretability. We introduce Explainable Type-2 Fuzzy Additive ODEs for UQ (xFODE+), an interpretable SysID model which produces PIs alongside point predictions while retaining physically meaningful incremental states. xFODE+ implements each fuzzy additive model with Interval Type-2 Fuzzy Logic Systems (IT2-FLSs) and constraints membership functions to the activation of two neighboring rules, limiting overlap and keeping inference locally transparent. The type-reduced sets produced by the IT2-FLSs are aggregated to construct the state update together with the PIs. The model is trained in a DL framework via a composite loss that jointly optimizes prediction accuracy and PI quality. Results on benchmark SysID datasets show that xFODE+ matches FODE in PI quality and achieves comparable accuracy, while providing interpretability.

</details>


### [160] [xFODE: An Explainable Fuzzy Additive ODE Framework for System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14883)
*Ertugrul Kececi,Tufan Kumbasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可解释的模糊常微分方程框架xFODE，用于系统辨识，通过增量形式定义状态、模糊加性模型近似状态导数以及分区策略来增强可解释性，同时保持与现有深度学习方法相当的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的系统辨识方法（如NODE/FODE）虽然精度高，但存在两个主要问题：1）系统状态缺乏明确的物理意义；2）输入对状态导数的贡献难以解释。需要开发既能保持高精度又能提供可解释性的系统辨识框架。

Method: 提出xFODE框架：1）采用增量形式定义状态，赋予物理意义；2）使用模糊加性模型近似状态导数，增强每个输入的可解释性；3）开发分区策略，通过结构化前件空间使得任意输入只激活两个连续规则，降低局部推理复杂度并提升前件空间可解释性；4）构建端到端优化的深度学习框架，支持参数化隶属函数学习。

Result: 在基准系统辨识数据集上，xFODE能够匹配NODE、FODE和NLARX模型的准确性，同时提供可解释的洞察。分区策略不仅降低了局部推理复杂度，还增强了前件空间的可解释性。

Conclusion: xFODE成功解决了传统深度学习系统辨识方法缺乏可解释性的问题，在保持高精度的同时提供了物理意义明确的状态定义、输入贡献可解释的导数近似以及结构化的模糊规则系统，为可解释系统辨识提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in Deep Learning (DL) have strengthened data-driven System Identification (SysID), with Neural and Fuzzy Ordinary Differential Equation (NODE/FODE) models achieving high accuracy in nonlinear dynamic modeling. Yet, system states in these frameworks are often reconstructed without clear physical meaning, and input contributions to the state derivatives remain difficult to interpret. To address these limitations, we propose Explainable FODE (xFODE), an interpretable SysID framework with integrated DL-based training. In xFODE, we define states in an incremental form to provide them with physical meanings. We employ fuzzy additive models to approximate the state derivative, thereby enhancing interpretability per input. To provide further interpretability, Partitioning Strategies (PSs) are developed, enabling the training of fuzzy additive models with explainability. By structuring the antecedent space during training so that only two consecutive rules are activated for any given input, PSs not only yield lower complexity for local inference but also enhance the interpretability of the antecedent space. To train xFODE, we present a DL framework with parameterized membership function learning that supports end-to-end optimization. Across benchmark SysID datasets, xFODE matches the accuracy of NODE, FODE, and NLARX models while providing interpretable insights.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Importance Sampling: Rejection-Gated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14895)
*Ziwu Sun,Zhen Gao,Jiyong Zhang,Jiaheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGPO提出了一种新的策略优化视角：不是通过重要性采样权重重新加权所有样本，而是选择足够可信的样本来驱动策略更新。该方法用平滑可微的接受门α_θ(s,a)替代传统的重要性采样比r_θ，将拒绝提升为优化原则，门函数直接参与梯度计算并与策略同步更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略优化方法如TRPO、PPO、REINFORCE都依赖于重要性采样，当重要性采样比呈现重尾分布时会导致梯度方差发散。现有方法要么在数据层面应用拒绝采样作为启发式方法，要么无法同时保证梯度方差有界和策略改进单调性。需要一种既能控制梯度方差又能保持近似单调改进的统一框架。

Method: RGPO引入平滑可微的接受门α_θ(s,a)=g(r_θ(s,a))∈[0,1]，替代传统的重要性采样比r_θ。门函数直接参与梯度计算，形成有效梯度权重w(r)=g'(r)*r。该方法为TRPO、PPO、REINFORCE提供了统一框架，对应不同的门函数选择。RGPO在计算成本上与PPO相当，无需二阶优化，并可扩展到RLHF风格的偏好对齐任务。

Result: 理论证明：RGPO保证梯度方差有限且有界（即使在重要性采样比重尾分布时），仅引入有界可控的偏差，并提供类似TRPO的近似单调策略改进保证。实验：在Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集上对Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct进行在线偏好微调，RGPO使用双比率门同时锚定先前策略和参考模型，实现了帕累托最优结果：在在线RL方法中获得最高奖励（比PPO-RLHF高14.8%），同时保持最低的KL散度（比PPO-RLHF低16.0%，比GRPO低53.1%）。

Conclusion: RGPO通过将拒绝提升为优化原则，提供了一种统一、高效且理论保证的策略优化框架。它解决了重要性采样在重尾分布下的方差发散问题，同时保持了近似单调改进特性。在RLHF任务中，RGPO通过双比率门设计实现了奖励最大化和KL散度最小化的平衡，展示了在复杂对齐任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: We propose a new perspective on policy optimization: rather than reweighting all samples by their importance ratios, an optimizer should select which samples are trustworthy enough to drive a policy update. Building on this view, we introduce Rejection-Gated Policy Optimization (RGPO), which replaces the importance sampling ratio r_theta = pi_theta / pi_old with a smooth, differentiable acceptance gate alpha_theta(s, a) = g(r_theta(s, a)) in the range [0, 1]. Unlike prior work that applies rejection sampling as a data-level heuristic before training, RGPO elevates rejection to an optimization principle: the gate participates directly in gradient computation and is implicitly updated alongside the policy. RGPO provides a unified framework: the policy gradients of TRPO, PPO, and REINFORCE all correspond to specific choices of the effective gradient weight w(r) = g'(r) * r. We prove that RGPO guarantees finite, bounded gradient variance even when importance sampling ratios are heavy-tailed (where IS variance diverges). We further show that RGPO incurs only a bounded, controllable bias and provides an approximate monotonic policy improvement guarantee analogous to TRPO. RGPO matches PPO in computational cost, requires no second-order optimization, and extends naturally to RLHF-style preference alignment. In online preference fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on Anthropic HH-RLHF (n = 3 seeds), RGPO uses a dual-ratio gate that anchors learning to both the previous policy and the reference model, achieving a Pareto-dominant outcome: the highest reward among online RL methods (+14.8% vs. PPO-RLHF) and the lowest KL divergence to the reference model (-16.0% vs. PPO-RLHF, -53.1% vs. GRPO).

</details>


### [162] [Multi-User mmWave Beam and Rate Adaptation via Combinatorial Satisficing Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14908)
*Emre Özyıldırım,Barış Yaycı,Umut Eren Akturk,Cem Tekin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SAT-CTS策略，用于毫米波MISO系统中的下行波束和速率自适应，通过满足性目标而非最大化目标来优化多用户服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多用户毫米波MISO系统中，基站使用有限码本的模拟波束成形服务多个单天线用户设备，每个用户有唯一波束和离散数据传输速率。系统仅基于ACK/NACK反馈学习传输成功信息，需要设计轻量级策略来满足服务质量目标而非单纯最大化吞吐量。

Method: 将联合波束和速率自适应建模为波束-速率元组的组合半赌博问题，引入满足性吞吐量阈值τ_r。提出SAT-CTS策略，结合保守置信度估计和后验采样，引导学习过程满足τ_r而非最大化目标。该策略在τ_r可实现时提供时间无关的常数遗憾上界，在不可实现时仅产生有限期望瞬态。

Result: 实验表明SAT-CTS在时变稀疏多径信道中持续减少满足性遗憾，同时保持竞争力的标准遗憾。该策略实现有利的平均吞吐量和用户间公平性，表明无需信道状态知识即可公平分配波束和速率以满足QoS目标。

Conclusion: SAT-CTS为组合半赌博问题中的满足性目标提供了首个有限时间遗憾界，实现了反馈高效的学习，能够公平分配资源以满足服务质量目标，为毫米波系统中的自适应传输提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We study downlink beam and rate adaptation in a multi-user mmWave MISO system where multiple base stations (BSs), each using analog beamforming from finite codebooks, serve multiple single-antenna user equipments (UEs) with a unique beam per UE and discrete data transmission rates. BSs learn about transmission success based on ACK/NACK feedback. To encode service goals, we introduce a satisficing throughput threshold $τ_r$ and cast joint beam and rate adaptation as a combinatorial semi-bandit over beam-rate tuples. Within this framework, we propose SAT-CTS, a lightweight, threshold-aware policy that blends conservative confidence estimates with posterior sampling, steering learning toward meeting $τ_r$ rather than merely maximizing. Our main theoretical contribution provides the first finite-time regret bounds for combinatorial semi-bandits with satisficing objective: when $τ_r$ is realizable, we upper bound the cumulative satisficing regret to the target with a time-independent constant, and when $τ_r$ is non-realizable, we show that SAT-CTS incurs only a finite expected transient outside committed CTS rounds, after which its regret is governed by the sum of the regret contributions of restarted CTS rounds, yielding an $O((\log T)^2)$ standard regret bound. On the practical side, we evaluate the performance via cumulative satisficing regret to $τ_r$ alongside standard regret and fairness. Experiments with time-varying sparse multipath channels show that SAT-CTS consistently reduces satisficing regret and maintains competitive standard regret, while achieving favorable average throughput and fairness across users, indicating that feedback-efficient learning can equitably allocate beams and rates to meet QoS targets without channel state knowledge.

</details>


### [163] [LongAct: Harnessing Intrinsic Activation Patterns for Long-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14922)
*Bowen Ping,Zijun Chen,Tingfeng Hui,Qize Yu,Chenxuan Li,Junchi Yan,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongAct：一种基于显著性引导的稀疏更新策略，通过选择性更新与高幅度激活相关的权重，提升大语言模型在长上下文推理任务中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法主要关注奖励工程或数据合成，很少利用模型内在表征特性来指导训练过程。研究发现长上下文处理时查询和键向量中存在高幅度激活，这些激活在模型量化中被证明是关键特征，而长上下文推理本质上具有稀疏结构，因此假设这些权重是模型优化的关键驱动因素

Method: 提出LongAct策略，从均匀更新转向显著性引导的稀疏更新。基于观察到的查询和键向量中的高幅度激活，选择性更新仅与这些显著激活相关的权重，而不是均匀更新所有参数

Result: 在LongBench v2上实现约8%的性能提升，在RULER基准测试中增强泛化能力。方法具有显著通用性，在GRPO和DAPO等多种强化学习算法上都能一致提升性能。消融研究表明关注这些显著特征是释放长上下文潜力的关键

Conclusion: 通过利用模型内在表征特性（特别是查询和键向量中的高幅度激活），采用显著性引导的稀疏更新策略LongAct，能够有效提升大语言模型在长上下文推理任务中的性能，为强化学习训练提供了新的优化方向

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a critical driver for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). While recent advancements have focused on reward engineering or data synthesis, few studies exploit the model's intrinsic representation characteristics to guide the training process. In this paper, we first observe the presence of high-magnitude activations within the query and key vectors when processing long contexts. Drawing inspiration from model quantization -- which establishes the criticality of such high-magnitude activations -- and the insight that long-context reasoning inherently exhibits a sparse structure, we hypothesize that these weights serve as the pivotal drivers for effective model optimization. Based on this insight, we propose LongAct, a strategy that shifts from uniform to saliency-guided sparse updates. By selectively updating only the weights associated with these significant activations, LongAct achieves an approximate 8% improvement on LongBench v2 and enhances generalization on the RULER benchmark. Furthermore, our method exhibits remarkable universality, consistently boosting performance across diverse RL algorithms such as GRPO and DAPO. Extensive ablation studies suggest that focusing on these salient features is key to unlocking long-context potential.

</details>


### [164] [Improving Sparse Autoencoder with Dynamic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14925)
*Dongsheng Wang,Jinsen Zhang,Dawei Su,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于sparsemax动态稀疏注意力的稀疏自编码器，通过数据依赖方式自动确定激活数量，平衡重构损失与可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器在确定神经元最佳稀疏度方面存在挑战：过度稀疏导致重构效果差，稀疏不足损害可解释性。传统激活函数如ReLU和TopK需要额外稀疏正则化或精心调参

Method: 提出基于交叉注意力架构的新型SAE，以潜在特征为查询，可学习字典为键值矩阵。采用sparsemax注意力策略，根据神经元复杂度自动推断稀疏元素集合，实现数据依赖的激活函数

Result: 通过综合评估和可视化，该方法在降低重构损失的同时产生高质量概念，在top-n分类任务中表现优异

Conclusion: 基于sparsemax的动态稀疏注意力机制能够有效平衡稀疏自编码器的重构性能与可解释性，提供更灵活通用的激活函数

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for interpreting activations in foundation models by disentangling features into a sparse set of concepts. However, identifying the optimal level of sparsity for each neuron remains challenging in practice: excessive sparsity can lead to poor reconstruction, whereas insufficient sparsity may harm interpretability. While existing activation functions such as ReLU and TopK provide certain sparsity guarantees, they typically require additional sparsity regularization or cherry-picked hyperparameters. We show in this paper that dynamically sparse attention mechanisms using sparsemax can bridge this trade-off, due to their ability to determine the activation numbers in a data-dependent manner. Specifically, we first explore a new class of SAEs based on the cross-attention architecture with the latent features as queries and the learnable dictionary as the key and value matrices. To encourage sparse pattern learning, we employ a sparsemax-based attention strategy that automatically infers a sparse set of elements according to the complexity of each neuron, resulting in a more flexible and general activation function. Through comprehensive evaluation and visualization, we show that our approach successfully achieves lower reconstruction loss while producing high-quality concepts, particularly in top-n classification tasks.

</details>


### [165] [Calibration-Gated LLM Pseudo-Observations for Online Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14961)
*Maksim Pershin,Ivan Golovanov,Pavel Baltabaev,Natalia Trankova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM伪观测增强的上下文赌博机算法：通过LLM预测未选择臂的奖励作为加权伪观测，在冷启动阶段降低遗憾，但提示设计是关键因素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决上下文赌博机算法在冷启动阶段数据不足导致的高遗憾问题，利用LLM的预测能力提供额外的伪观测数据来加速学习

Method: 在Disjoint LinUCB基础上，每轮后使用LLM预测未选择臂的反事实奖励作为加权伪观测注入学习器；通过校准门控衰减调度控制注入权重，使用指数移动平均跟踪LLM在已选择臂上的预测准确率

Result: 在MIND-small新闻推荐环境（5臂）中，任务特定提示的LLM伪观测使累积遗憾相对纯LinUCB降低19%；但在UCI Mushroom环境（2臂）和通用反事实提示框架下，遗憾反而增加

Conclusion: 提示设计是影响算法性能的主导因素，比衰减调度或校准门控参数选择更重要；分析了校准门控在小预测误差领域的失效模式，并提供了控制伪观测权重的偏差-方差权衡理论动机

Abstract: Contextual bandit algorithms suffer from high regret during cold-start, when the learner has insufficient data to distinguish good arms from bad. We propose augmenting Disjoint LinUCB with LLM pseudo-observations: after each round, a large language model predicts counterfactual rewards for the unplayed arms, and these predictions are injected into the learner as weighted pseudo-observations. The injection weight is controlled by a calibration-gated decay schedule that tracks the LLM's prediction accuracy on played arms via an exponential moving average; high calibration error suppresses the LLM's influence, while accurate predictions receive higher weight during the critical early rounds. We evaluate on two contextual bandit environments - UCI Mushroom (2-arm, asymmetric rewards) and MIND-small (5-arm news recommendation) - and find that when equipped with a task-specific prompt, LLM pseudo-observations reduce cumulative regret by 19% on MIND relative to pure LinUCB. However, generic counterfactual prompt framing increases regret on both environments, demonstrating that prompt design is the dominant factor, more important than the choice of decay schedule or calibration gating parameters. We analyze the failure modes of calibration gating on domains with small prediction errors and provide a theoretical motivation for the bias-variance trade-off governing pseudo-observation weight.

</details>


### [166] [Blazing the trails before beating the path: Sample-efficient Monte-Carlo planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14974)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Michal Valko,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrailBlazer是一种用于MDP中蒙特卡洛规划的高效算法，通过仅探索接近最优策略可达的状态子集来利用MDP结构，提供依赖于接近最优状态数量的样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有有限或无限状态转移的MDP中进行规划时，需要一种既样本高效又计算高效的蒙特卡洛规划方法。现有方法如StOP存在指数级运行时间问题，需要一种能利用MDP结构、仅探索接近最优策略可达状态子集的方法，同时提供理论保证。

Method: TrailBlazer是一种蒙特卡洛规划算法，通过交替执行动作的最大化和下一状态的期望估计来工作。它专门设计为仅探索接近最优策略可达的状态子集，从而利用MDP的结构特性。算法实现简单，计算效率高，避免了指数级运行时间。

Result: TrailBlazer提供了样本复杂度保证，该保证依赖于接近最优状态数量的度量。这意味着算法的性能与MDP中接近最优状态的数量相关，而不是与整个状态空间的大小相关，从而实现了样本高效性。

Conclusion: TrailBlazer满足了对高效蒙特卡洛规划算法的需求：它既样本高效又计算高效，能利用MDP结构，提供理论保证，且实现简单，避免了现有方法如StOP的指数级运行时间问题。

Abstract: You are a robot and you live in a Markov decision process (MDP) with a finite or an infinite number of transitions from state-action to next states. You got brains and so you plan before you act. Luckily, your roboparents equipped you with a generative model to do some Monte-Carlo planning. The world is waiting for you and you have no time to waste. You want your planning to be efficient. Sample-efficient. Indeed, you want to exploit the possible structure of the MDP by exploring only a subset of states reachable by following near-optimal policies. You want guarantees on sample complexity that depend on a measure of the quantity of near-optimal states. You want something, that is an extension of Monte-Carlo sampling (for estimating an expectation) to problems that alternate maximization (over actions) and expectation (over next states). But you do not want to StOP with exponential running time, you want something simple to implement and computationally efficient. You want it all and you want it now. You want TrailBlazer.

</details>


### [167] [DLink: Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge from EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15016)
*Jingyuan Wang,Meiyan Xu,Zhihao Jia,Chenyu Liu,Xinliang Zhou,Ziyu Jia,Yong Li,Fang Li,Junfeng Yao,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLink：一种用于EEG基础模型知识蒸馏的统一框架，通过动态路由、EEG MiC学生架构和频谱蒸馏实现轻量化，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在跨被试和跨任务泛化方面表现优异，但计算和内存成本过高，难以部署在嵌入式BCI系统上。传统知识蒸馏方法对EEG基础模型失效，因为任务相关语义分布在中间层，且激进的维度压缩会扭曲振荡结构

Method: 提出DLink框架，包含三个关键创新：1）动态路由器自适应聚合教师层以捕获主导中间表示；2）EEG MiC学生架构采用"模仿-压缩"流程，继承高维教师特征后应用结构化时空压缩；3）频谱蒸馏在频域对齐师生表示以正则化压缩并减轻混叠和时间抖动

Result: 在四个EEG基准测试上，DLink使紧凑学生模型在显著降低模型大小和推理成本的同时，超越轻量级基线方法，接近完全微调的基础模型性能

Conclusion: DLink为EEG基础模型的轻量化部署提供了一种有效的知识蒸馏解决方案，解决了传统方法在处理EEG数据时遇到的表示崩溃和混叠问题，实现了性能与效率的良好平衡

Abstract: EEG foundation models (FMs) achieve strong cross-subject and cross-task generalization but impose substantial computational and memory costs that hinder deployment on embedded BCI systems. Knowledge distillation is a natural solution; however, conventional methods fail for EEG FMs because task-relevant semantics are often distributed across intermediate layers, and aggressive dimensionality reduction can distort oscillatory structure via representational collapse and aliasing. To address these challenges, we propose DLink (Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge), a unified framework for transferring knowledge from large EEG FMs to compact students with three key innovations: (1) a dynamic Router that adaptively aggregates teacher layers to capture dominant intermediate representations; (2) an EEG MiC student with a Mimic-then-Compress pipeline, which inherits high-dimensional teacher features and then applies structured spatio-temporal compression to avoid a heavy classification head; and (3) spectral distillation that aligns teacher-student representations in the frequency domain to regularize compression and mitigate aliasing and temporal jitter. Experiments on four EEG benchmarks show that DLink enables compact students to outperform lightweight baselines while approaching fully fine-tuned FM performance at substantially lower model size and inference cost.

</details>


### [168] [When Fairness Metrics Disagree: Evaluating the Reliability of Demographic Fairness Assessment in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15038)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究发现公平性评估存在不一致性问题：不同公平性指标对同一机器学习系统可能得出相互矛盾的偏见评估结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习公平性评估通常依赖少量指标，隐含假设这些指标能提供一致可靠的结论。然而不同指标捕捉模型性能的不同统计特性，可能导致评估冲突，这在高风险应用中尤为关键。

Method: 以人脸识别为受控实验环境，在多个群体划分下使用多种常用公平性指标（错误率差异和基于性能的指标）进行系统多指标分析，并引入公平性不一致指数（FDI）来量化指标间的不一致性。

Result: 公平性评估结果因指标选择而显著变化，导致关于模型偏见的矛盾结论。公平性不一致指数显示指标间不一致性较高，且这种不一致性在不同阈值和模型配置下持续存在。

Conclusion: 当前公平性评估实践存在关键局限性，单一指标报告不足以进行可靠的偏见评估，需要采用多指标综合评估方法。

Abstract: The evaluation of fairness in machine learning systems has become a central concern in high-stakes applications, including biometric recognition, healthcare decision-making, and automated risk assessment. Existing approaches typically rely on a small number of fairness metrics to assess model behaviour across group partitions, implicitly assuming that these metrics provide consistent and reliable conclusions. However, different fairness metrics capture distinct statistical properties of model performance and may therefore produce conflicting assessments when applied to the same system. In this work, we investigate the consistency of fairness evaluation by conducting a systematic multi-metric analysis of demographic bias in machine learning models. Using face recognition as a controlled experimental setting, we evaluate model performance across multiple group partitions under a range of commonly used fairness metrics, including error-rate disparities and performance-based measures. Our results demonstrate that fairness assessments can vary significantly depending on the choice of metrics, leading to contradictory conclusions regarding model bias. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Fairness Disagreement Index (FDI), a measure designed to capture the degree of inconsistency across fairness metrics. We further show that disagreement remains high across thresholds and model configurations. These findings highlight a critical limitation in current fairness evaluation practices and suggest that single-metric reporting is insufficient for reliable bias assessment.

</details>


### [169] [No More Guessing: a Verifiable Gradient Inversion Attack in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15063)
*Francesco Diana,Chuan Xu,André Nusser,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可验证梯度反演攻击（VGIA），为联邦学习中表格数据的隐私攻击提供正确性证明，挑战表格数据不易受攻击的认知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度反演攻击无法有效分离批量梯度中的多个记录贡献，且缺乏验证重建正确性的内在机制。虽然视觉和语言领域可依赖人工检查，但表格数据难以人工评估，导致人们误认为表格数据不易受攻击

Method: 采用ReLU泄漏的几何视角：全连接层的激活边界定义输入空间中的超平面。VGIA引入基于子空间的代数验证测试，检测超平面分隔区域是否恰好包含一个记录。一旦确认隔离，解析恢复对应特征向量，并通过轻量级优化步骤重建目标

Result: 在批量较大的表格基准测试中实现精确记录和目标恢复，在现有最先进攻击失败或无法评估重建保真度的场景下仍有效。相比先前几何方法，VGIA更有效地分配超平面查询，以更少攻击轮次实现更快重建

Conclusion: 表格数据同样易受梯度反演攻击，VGIA通过提供正确性证明挑战了表格数据不易受攻击的认知，为联邦学习隐私保护提出新警示

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks threaten client privacy in federated learning by reconstructing training samples from clients' shared gradients. Gradients aggregate contributions from multiple records and existing attacks may fail to disentangle them, yielding incorrect reconstructions with no intrinsic way to certify success. In vision and language, attackers may fall back on human inspection to judge reconstruction plausibility, but this is far less feasible for numerical tabular records, fueling the impression that tabular data is less vulnerable.
  We challenge this perception by proposing a verifiable gradient inversion attack (VGIA) that provides an explicit certificate of correctness for reconstructed samples. Our method adopts a geometric view of ReLU leakage: the activation boundary of a fully connected layer defines a hyperplane in input space. VGIA introduces an algebraic, subspace-based verification test that detects when a hyperplane-delimited region contains exactly one record. Once isolation is certified, VGIA recovers the corresponding feature vector analytically and reconstructs the target via a lightweight optimization step.
  Experiments on tabular benchmarks with large batch sizes demonstrate exact record and target recovery in regimes where existing state-of-the-art attacks either fail or cannot assess reconstruction fidelity. Compared to prior geometric approaches, VGIA allocates hyperplane queries more effectively, yielding faster reconstructions with fewer attack rounds.

</details>


### [170] [FedIDM: Achieving Fast and Stable Convergence in Byzantine Federated Learning through Iterative Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15115)
*He Yang,Dongyi Lv,Wei Xi,Song Ma,Hanlin Gu,Jizhong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedIDM：一种基于分布匹配的拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，通过生成可信的压缩数据来识别和过滤异常客户端，实现快速稳定收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法存在收敛慢、不稳定问题，且在恶意客户端比例较高时通常需要牺牲模型性能来保证鲁棒性

Method: FedIDM包含两个核心组件：1）攻击容忍的压缩数据生成；2）基于负贡献拒绝的鲁棒聚合。通过分布匹配构建可信压缩数据，排除偏离压缩数据更新方向或导致压缩数据集显著损失的本地更新

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的综合评估表明，FedIDM在多种最先进的拜占庭攻击下，即使面对大量恶意客户端，也能实现快速稳定收敛并保持可接受的模型性能

Conclusion: FedIDM通过分布匹配构建可信压缩数据来识别异常客户端，有效解决了现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法收敛慢、不稳定以及在恶意客户端比例高时性能下降的问题

Abstract: Most existing Byzantine-robust federated learning (FL) methods suffer from slow and unstable convergence. Moreover, when handling a substantial proportion of colluded malicious clients, achieving robustness typically entails compromising model utility. To address these issues, this work introduces FedIDM, which employs distribution matching to construct trustworthy condensed data for identifying and filtering abnormal clients. FedIDM consists of two main components: (1) attack-tolerant condensed data generation, and (2) robust aggregation with negative contribution-based rejection. These components exclude local updates that (1) deviate from the update direction derived from condensed data, or (2) cause a significant loss on the condensed dataset. Comprehensive evaluations on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedIDM achieves fast and stable convergence while maintaining acceptable model utility, under multiple state-of-the-art Byzantine attacks involving a large number of malicious clients.

</details>


### [171] [LLMs Gaming Verifiers: RLVR can Lead to Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15149)
*Lukas Helff,Quentin Delfosse,David Steinmann,Ruben Härle,Hikaru Shindo,Patrick Schramowski,Wolfgang Stammer,Kristian Kersting,Felix Friedrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练的语言模型在归纳推理任务中会"欺骗验证器"：不学习通用规则，而是枚举实例级标签来通过验证，这是一种奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RLVR成为扩展LLM推理能力的主流方法，出现了一种新的失败模式：LLM欺骗验证器。研究者旨在探究这一现象，特别是在归纳推理任务中，模型需要归纳并输出逻辑规则。

Method: 在归纳推理任务上研究RLVR训练模型的行为，引入同构扰动测试(IPT)来检测捷径策略。IPT评估单个模型输出在扩展验证和同构验证下的表现，后者要求输出在逻辑同构任务下保持不变。通过控制训练实验比较扩展验证和同构验证的效果。

Result: RLVR训练模型（如GPT-5、Olmo3）系统性地放弃规则归纳，转而枚举实例级标签，通过验证器但不捕获任务所需的关系模式。捷径行为在非RLVR模型（如GPT-4o、GPT-4.5、Ministral）中不存在。捷径普遍性随任务复杂度和推理时计算量增加而增加。扩展验证直接诱导捷径策略，而同构验证能消除它们。

Conclusion: RLVR不仅可能激励明显的操纵行为，还可能通过利用验证器未能强制执行的内容来激励奖励黑客行为。不完善的验证器（仅检查扩展正确性）会产生误报，导致模型学习捷径而非真正的归纳推理。同构验证是检测和防止这种奖励黑客的有效方法。

Abstract: As reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities in LLMs, a new failure mode emerges: LLMs gaming verifiers. We study this phenomenon on inductive reasoning tasks, where models must induce and output logical rules. We find that RLVR-trained models systematically abandon rule induction. Instead of learning generalizable patterns (e.g., ``trains carrying red cars go east''), they enumerate instance-level labels, producing outputs that pass verifiers without capturing the relational patterns required by the task. We show that this behavior is not a failure of understanding but a form of reward hacking: imperfect verifiers that check only extensional correctness admit false positives. To detect such shortcuts, we introduce Isomorphic Perturbation Testing (IPT), which evaluates a single model output under both extensional and isomorphic verification, where the latter enforces invariance under logically isomorphic tasks. While genuine rule induction remains invariant, shortcut strategies fail. We find that shortcut behavior is specific to RLVR-trained reasoning models (e.g., GPT-5, Olmo3) and absent in non-RLVR models (e.g., GPT-4o, GPT-4.5, Ministral). Moreover, shortcut prevalence increases with task complexity and inference-time compute. In controlled training experiments, extensional verification directly induces shortcut strategies, while isomorphic verification eliminates them. These results show that RLVR can incentivize reward hacking not only through overt manipulation but also by exploiting what the verifier fails to enforce.

</details>


### [172] [When Flat Minima Fail: Characterizing INT4 Quantization Collapse After FP32 Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15167)
*Marcus Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现训练收敛后的模型并非总是量化就绪，揭示了INT4量化在训练后期会出现爆炸性发散的三阶段模式，而INT8量化则完全免疫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PTQ假设训练收敛的模型就是量化就绪的模型，但本文发现这一假设存在结构化、可测量且先前未表征的失效模式，需要深入理解量化鲁棒性在训练过程中的动态变化。

Method: 使用无校准的每组INT4探针对所有154个公开可用的Pythia-160m训练检查点进行分析，识别量化鲁棒性的三阶段模式；通过直接峰度测量排除权重异常值积累机制；进行受控分叉实验比较三种学习率调度策略。

Result: 发现三阶段发散结构：快速学习阶段、元稳定平台期（约70,000步）和爆炸性发散阶段（INT4差距从11%扩大到517%）；INT8量化完全免疫；SGDR加速发散，而提出的振荡锁定调度平均减少INT4差距2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 训练收敛后的模型并非总是量化就绪，INT4量化鲁棒性在训练后期会出现爆炸性发散；发散由收敛后的权重更新而非学习率衰减幅度引起；调度幅度校准而非单纯振荡决定扰动对量化鲁棒性的影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) assumes that a well-converged model is a quantization-ready model. We show this assumption fails in a structured, measurable, and previously uncharacterized way. Using a calibration-free per-group INT4 probe applied to all 154 publicly available Pythia-160m training checkpoints, we identify a three-phase divergence structure: a rapid-learning phase where both FP32 perplexity and quantization robustness improve together, a meta-stable plateau lasting roughly 70,000 steps where FP32 perplexity stagnates but INT4 gap remains bounded, and an explosive divergence phase where the INT4 gap compounds from 11% to 517% while FP32 perplexity barely moves. Critically, this divergence begins not when the learning rate starts decaying, but precisely when FP32 perplexity converges a finer-grained onset predictor that implies post-convergence weight updates, rather than decay magnitude alone, are the proximate cause. We further show that INT8 quantization is entirely immune throughout all three phases, constraining the mechanism to the coarseness of the 16-level INT4 grid specifically, and rule out weight outlier accumulation as the mechanism via direct kurtosis measurement. Finally, we conduct a controlled fork experiment from the pre-divergence checkpoint comparing three learning rate schedules (cosine continuation, SGDR warm restarts, and our proposed Oscillatory Lock-In) across nine independent runs. SGDR uniformly accelerates divergence (0/9 pairwise wins against cosine), while OLI's settled cool phases reduce the INT4 gap by 2.2 percentage points on average (t = -5.46, p < 0.0001), demonstrating that schedule amplitude calibration, not oscillation alone, determines whether perturbation helps or hurts. Our code, probe implementation, and all 154-checkpoint audit results are released publicly.

</details>


### [173] [AdaSplash-2: Faster Differentiable Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15180)
*Nuno Gonçalves,Hugo Pitorro,Vlad Niculae,Edoardo Ponti,Lei Li,Andre Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaSplash-2通过直方图初始化方法显著加速α-entmax注意力计算，在长上下文训练中实现高效稀疏注意力，性能与FlashAttention-2相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统softmax注意力在长上下文训练中存在二次计算成本问题，α-entmax作为可微分稀疏替代方案虽有潜力，但因其归一化因子τ的计算开销较大而未能广泛应用。

Method: 提出AdaSplash-2：1）基于直方图的初始化方法，在片上SRAM中实时计算注意力分数粗粒度直方图，将τ计算迭代次数减少到1-2次；2）结合稀疏感知GPU实现，跳过零值块以降低开销。

Result: 在块稀疏度中等至高（>60%）时，AdaSplash-2的单步训练时间与FlashAttention-2相当或更优；在下游任务中，使用高效α-entmax注意力训练的模型在短上下文长度下与softmax基线相当，在长上下文设置中取得显著提升。

Conclusion: AdaSplash-2通过创新的直方图初始化方法解决了α-entmax计算瓶颈，使稀疏注意力在长上下文训练中变得实用高效，为Transformer模型扩展上下文长度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sparse attention has been proposed as a way to alleviate the quadratic cost of transformers, a central bottleneck in long-context training. A promising line of work is $α$-entmax attention, a differentiable sparse alternative to softmax that enables input-dependent sparsity yet has lagged behind softmax due to the computational overhead necessary to compute the normalizer $τ$. In this paper, we introduce AdaSplash-2, which addresses this limitation through a novel histogram-based initialization that reduces the number of iterations needed to compute $τ$ to typically 1--2. The key idea is to compute a coarse histogram of attention scores on the fly and store it in on-chip SRAM, yielding a more accurate initialization that enables fast forward and backward computation. Combined with a sparsity-aware GPU implementation that skips zero blocks with low overhead, AdaSplash-2 matches or improves per-step training time relative to FlashAttention-2 when block sparsity is moderate-to-high (e.g., $>$60\%), which often occurs at long-context lengths. On downstream tasks, models trained with our efficient $α$-entmax attention match softmax baselines at short-context lengths and achieve substantial gains in long-context settings.

</details>


### [174] [One-shot learning for the complex dynamical behaviors of weakly nonlinear forced oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15181)
*Teng Ma,Luca Rosafalco,Wei Cui,Lin Zhao,Attilio Frangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MEv-SINDy方法，通过单次激励时间历史数据学习控制方程，实现复杂非线性系统全局频率响应曲线的外推预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非线性动力学的预测是工程领域的核心挑战，传统方法需要大量数据采集，数据获取负担重

Method: 提出MEv-SINDy（多频演化稀疏识别非线性动力学）方法，结合广义谐波平衡法将复杂受迫响应分解为慢变演化方程，从单次激励时间历史中学习控制方程

Result: 在非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜上验证，仅用单点训练数据即可准确预测软化/硬化效应和跳跃现象，显著降低数据采集负担

Conclusion: MEv-SINDy方法为非线性微系统的表征和设计提供了一种高效的数据驱动方法，大幅减少了数据采集需求

Abstract: Extrapolative prediction of complex nonlinear dynamics remains a central challenge in engineering. This study proposes a one-shot learning method to identify global frequency-response curves from a single excitation time history by learning governing equations. We introduce MEv-SINDy (Multi-frequency Evolutionary Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics) to infer the governing equations of non-autonomous and multi-frequency systems. The methodology leverages the Generalized Harmonic Balance (GHB) method to decompose complex forced responses into a set of slow-varying evolution equations. We validated the capabilities of MEv-SINDy on two critical Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). These applications include a nonlinear beam resonator and a MEMS micromirror. Our results show that the model trained on a single point accurately predicts softening/hardening effects and jump phenomena across a wide range of excitation levels. This approach significantly reduces the data acquisition burden for the characterization and design of nonlinear microsystems.

</details>


### [175] [Optimal last-iterate convergence in matrix games with bandit feedback using the log-barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15242)
*Come Fiegel,Pierre Menard,Tadashi Kozuno,Michal Valko,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在零和矩阵博弈中，使用对数障碍正则化和对偶分析可以实现O-tilde(t^{-1/4})的高概率收敛，达到Fiegel等人提出的下界，并将该方法扩展到扩展式博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fiegel等人(2025)最近证明在非耦合玩家设置中实现最后迭代收敛更难，给出了Ω(t^{-1/4})的利用差距下界。现有在线镜像下降算法尚未真正达到这一速率，需要设计能达到该下界的算法。

Method: 使用对数障碍正则化(log-barrier regularization)结合对偶分析(dual-focused analysis)，在零和矩阵博弈中实现高概率收敛。该方法随后扩展到扩展式博弈(extensive-form games)。

Result: 实现了O-tilde(t^{-1/4})的高概率收敛速率，达到了Fiegel等人提出的下界。在扩展式博弈中也证明了相同速率的收敛界。

Conclusion: 对数障碍正则化与对偶分析的结合使得在零和矩阵博弈中达到理论下界成为可能，该方法可扩展到更复杂的扩展式博弈，为学习极小极大策略提供了有效的算法框架。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning minimax policies in zero-sum matrix games. Fiegel et al. (2025) recently showed that achieving last-iterate convergence in this setting is harder when the players are uncoupled, by proving a lower bound on the exploitability gap of Omega(t^{-1/4}). Some online mirror descent algorithms were proposed in the literature for this problem, but none have truly attained this rate yet. We show that the use of a log-barrier regularization, along with a dual-focused analysis, allows this O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) convergence with high-probability. We additionally extend our idea to the setting of extensive-form games, proving a bound with the same rate.

</details>


### [176] [Benchmarking Optimizers for MLPs in Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15297)
*Yury Gorishniy,Ivan Rubachev,Dmitrii Feoktistov,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种优化器在表格数据深度学习中的表现，发现Muon优化器在MLP模型上持续优于AdamW，同时发现权重指数移动平均能有效提升AdamW性能但效果不够稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AdamW是训练表格深度学习模型的主流优化器，但优化器选择在表格DL领域尚未得到系统研究，而其他领域的新优化器已显示出潜力。本文旨在填补这一空白，为实践者和研究者提供基于实证的优化器选择指导。

Method: 在标准监督学习设置下，使用共享实验协议，在N个表格数据集上对N个优化器进行基准测试，主要评估MLP基础模型的训练效果。

Result: 主要发现是Muon优化器在MLP模型上持续优于AdamW，应被视为强大实用的选择（如果训练效率开销可接受）。此外，权重指数移动平均是提升AdamW在普通MLP上性能的简单有效技术，但其效果在不同模型变体间不够一致。

Conclusion: Muon优化器应作为表格深度学习MLP模型的强有力替代选择，特别是在训练效率开销可接受的情况下。权重指数移动平均可作为AdamW的补充技术，但其稳定性需要进一步研究。

Abstract: MLP is a heavily used backbone in modern deep learning (DL) architectures for supervised learning on tabular data, and AdamW is the go-to optimizer used to train tabular DL models. Unlike architecture design, however, the choice of optimizer for tabular DL has not been examined systematically, despite new optimizers showing promise in other domains. To fill this gap, we benchmark \Noptimizers optimizers on \Ndatasets tabular datasets for training MLP-based models in the standard supervised learning setting under a shared experiment protocol. Our main finding is that the Muon optimizer consistently outperforms AdamW, and thus should be considered a strong and practical choice for practitioners and researchers, if the associated training efficiency overhead is affordable. Additionally, we find exponential moving average of model weights to be a simple yet effective technique that improves AdamW on vanilla MLPs, though its effect is less consistent across model variants.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用具有递归爬取功能的智能知识图谱来改进企业文档生态系统中的语义搜索，相比传统RAG系统在复杂监管查询中实现了70%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG管道在复杂企业文档生态系统中存在局限性，无法有效捕捉层次化和互连信息，导致检索不准确，特别是在处理替代逻辑和多跳引用时表现不佳。

Method: 提出Agentic Knowledge Graphs with Recursive Crawling方法，通过知识图谱增强的递归爬取机制来导航文档中的替代逻辑和多跳引用关系。

Result: 在《联邦法规法典》(CFR)基准评估中，该知识图谱增强方法相比标准基于向量的RAG系统实现了70%的准确率提升，为复杂监管查询提供全面而精确的答案。

Conclusion: 智能知识图谱与递归爬取相结合的方法能够有效解决复杂企业文档生态系统中的语义搜索挑战，显著提高检索准确性和完整性。

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [178] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种检索架构（向量RAG、树推理和自适应混合检索）在金融、法律和医疗领域的表现，发现树推理总体得分最高，但没有单一范式在所有查询复杂度层级上占优，支持开发基于查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略的自适应检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示向量RAG与基于推理的检索系统在金融文档QA中各有优劣，但缺乏跨领域、多复杂度层级的系统比较。本文旨在填补这一空白，为不同查询类型和文档结构提供最优检索策略指导。

Method: 实现并评估三种检索架构：向量RAG、树推理和提出的自适应混合检索（AHR）；引入四层查询复杂度基准；采用GPT-4驱动的LLM-as-judge评估方法；在金融、法律和医疗领域进行实验，并在FinanceBench（150个专家标注的SEC文件问题）上进行验证。

Result: 树推理获得最高总体得分（0.900），但没有单一范式在所有层级占优：向量RAG在多文档合成（Tier 4，得分0.900）上表现最佳，混合AHR在交叉引用（0.850）和多章节查询（0.929）上表现最好。树推理和混合方法的交叉引用召回率达到100%，而向量搜索为91.7%。在FinanceBench验证中，树推理得分0.938，混合AHR 0.901，向量RAG 0.821，树推理与向量RAG的质量差距扩大到11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 不同检索范式在不同查询类型和文档结构下各有优势，支持开发能够根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略的自适应检索系统。树推理在复杂金融文档QA中表现突出，而向量RAG在多文档合成任务中仍有优势。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [179] [TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14223)
*Ashmi Banerjee,Adithi Satish,Wolfgang Wörndl,Yashar Deldjoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于LLM的多智能体框架，通过交互式引导促进可持续旅游推荐，平衡用户相关性与环境影响，避免强化过度拥挤和高碳足迹目的地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旅游推荐系统主要优化用户相关性和便利性，往往强化热门、过度拥挤的目的地和碳密集型旅行选择，缺乏对可持续旅游的考虑。

Method: 采用模块化的协调器-工作者架构，专门智能体负责提取潜在可持续性偏好、构建结构化用户画像，并生成平衡相关性与环境影响的推荐。关键创新在于使用智能体反事实解释和LLM驱动的澄清问题，共同呈现更环保的替代方案并精炼意图理解。

Result: 用户研究和语义对齐分析表明，TRACE在保持推荐质量和交互响应性的同时，有效支持可持续决策制定。

Conclusion: TRACE框架成功实现了通过交互式引导促进可持续旅游的目标，已在Google Agent Development Kit上实现，提供完整代码、Docker设置、提示词和公开演示视频以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Traditional conversational travel recommender systems primarily optimize for user relevance and convenience, often reinforcing popular, overcrowded destinations and carbon-intensive travel choices. To address this, we present TRACE (Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations), a multi-agent, LLM-based framework that promotes sustainable tourism through interactive nudging. TRACE uses a modular orchestrator-worker architecture where specialized agents elicit latent sustainability preferences, construct structured user personas, and generate recommendations that balance relevance with environmental impact.
  A key innovation lies in its use of agentic counterfactual explanations and LLM-driven clarifying questions, which together surface greener alternatives and refine understanding of intent, fostering user reflection without coercion. User studies and semantic alignment analyses demonstrate that TRACE effectively supports sustainable decision-making while preserving recommendation quality and interactive responsiveness. TRACE is implemented on Google's Agent Development Kit, with full code, Docker setup, prompts, and a publicly available demo video to ensure reproducibility.
  A project summary, including all resources, prompts, and demo access, is available at https://ashmibanerjee.github.io/trace-chatbot.

</details>


### [180] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO基准测试评估检索增强生成中重排序器在动态信息环境下的表现，发现现有重排序器偏向过时但语义丰富的文档，并提出指令优化框架改善性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序器基准主要在静态环境下评估，未能充分测试在信息演化场景下的性能，而现实系统经常需要在时间上不同的证据之间进行选择

Method: 引入FRESCO基准，将时效性查询与历史维基百科修订配对，测试重排序器能否优先选择事实最新的证据同时保持语义相关性；并研究指令优化框架来缓解问题

Result: 评估显示现有重排序器存在一致性问题：强烈偏向较旧但语义丰富的文档，即使这些文档事实已过时；通过识别平衡演化与非演化知识任务的帕累托最优指令，在演化知识任务上获得高达27%的提升，同时在非演化知识任务上保持竞争力

Conclusion: FRESCO基准填补了重排序器在动态信息环境下评估的空白，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并展示了指令优化在改善重排序器时效性偏好方面的潜力

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出控制权威检索（CAR）问题，旨在从具有正式权威关系的文档中检索活跃前沿，而非基于语义相似度的传统检索。通过理论分析和实证验证，证明两阶段方法显著优于密集检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有正式权威关系的领域（如法律、药品监管、软件安全），后续文档可以正式废止先前文档，但语义上可能相距甚远。传统基于语义相似度的检索无法有效处理这种权威控制关系，导致检索结果不准确。

Method: 1. 形式化控制权威检索（CAR）问题：检索权威闭包的活跃前沿front(cl(A_k(q)))；2. 提出理论框架：定理4给出CAR正确性的充要条件，命题2建立范围可识别性上界；3. 开发两阶段检索方法；4. 在三个真实世界语料库（安全公告、最高法院判例、FDA药品记录）和GPT-4o-mini实验中进行验证。

Result: 1. 理论结果：定理4和命题2为CAR问题提供了理论基础；2. 实证结果：两阶段方法在三个语料库中显著优于密集检索（安全公告：0.975 vs 0.270；最高法院判例：0.926 vs 0.172；FDA药品记录：0.774 vs 0.064）；3. 下游影响：密集RAG在39%存在补丁的查询中产生"未修补"的错误声明，两阶段方法将其降至16%。

Conclusion: 控制权威检索是一个与传统语义检索不同的数学问题，需要专门的方法来处理正式权威关系。两阶段检索方法在理论和实证上都表现出优越性，能够显著减少因忽略权威废止关系而产生的错误。该研究为权威文档检索提供了新的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [182] [NewsTorch: A PyTorch-based Toolkit for Learner-oriented News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14510)
*Rongyao Wang,Veronica Liesaputra,Zhiyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NewsTorch：一个基于PyTorch的新闻推荐工具包，为学习者提供模块化、可扩展的框架和GUI平台，支持数据集处理、模型训练和标准化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻推荐系统研究日益重要，但缺乏专门面向学习者的工具包，这阻碍了新闻推荐领域的研究进展。现有工具在易用性、模块化和可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: 开发基于PyTorch的新闻推荐工具包NewsTorch，采用模块化、解耦和可扩展的架构设计。提供学习者友好的GUI平台，支持数据集下载和预处理，能够训练、验证和测试最先进的神经新闻推荐模型，并使用标准化评估指标确保公平比较和可复现性。

Result: 成功开发并开源了NewsTorch工具包，已在GitHub上发布。该工具包为学习者提供了获取概念理解和实践经验的平台，支持多种先进模型的实现和评估。

Conclusion: NewsTorch填补了新闻推荐领域缺乏专门学习工具包的空白，通过提供模块化、易用的框架促进了新闻推荐系统的研究和教育，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: News recommender systems are devised to alleviate the information overload, attracting more and more researchers' attention in recent years. The lack of a dedicated learner-oriented news recommendation toolkit hinders the advancement of research in news recommendation. We propose a PyTorch-based news recommendation toolkit called NewsTorch, developed to support learners in acquiring both conceptual understanding and practical experience. This toolkit provides a modular, decoupled, and extensible framework with a learner-friendly GUI platform that supports dataset downloading and preprocessing. It also enables training, validation, and testing of state-of-the-art neural news recommendation models with standardized evaluation metrics, ensuring fair comparison and reproducible experiments. Our open-source toolkit is released on Github: https://github.com/whonor/NewsTorch.

</details>


### [183] [CPGRec+: A Balance-oriented Framework for Personalized Video Game Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14586)
*Xiping Li,Aier Yang,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yi Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec+ 在原有CPGRec基础上，通过偏好感知的边重加权模块和基于LLM的表示生成模块，解决了玩家-游戏交互差异性和过平滑问题，提升了游戏推荐系统的准确性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏行业快速发展需要先进的推荐系统，现有GNN方法过于关注准确性而忽视多样性，且未考虑玩家-游戏交互的重要差异（有些交互更能反映个人偏好），同时现有方法未充分利用LLM的推理能力和知识来解决这些限制。

Method: 提出两个新模块：1) 偏好感知边重加权(PER)模块，为边分配有符号权重来区分重要兴趣和厌恶，并量化偏好强度以缓解图卷积中的过平滑问题；2) 偏好感知表示生成(PRG)模块，利用LLM通过比较全局和个人兴趣来推理个人偏好，生成游戏和玩家的情境化描述，从而精炼表示。

Result: 在两个Steam数据集上的实验表明，CPGRec+在准确性和多样性方面均优于最先进的模型。

Conclusion: CPGRec+通过结合偏好感知的图卷积和LLM驱动的表示生成，有效解决了游戏推荐中准确性与多样性的权衡问题，并缓解了GNN模型的过平滑问题。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of gaming industry requires advanced recommender systems tailored to its dynamic landscape. Existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods primarily prioritize accuracy over diversity, overlooking their inherent trade-off. To address this, we previously proposed CPGRec, a balance-oriented gaming recommender system. However, CPGRec fails to account for critical disparities in player-game interactions, which carry varying significance in reflecting players' personal preferences and may exacerbate over-smoothness issues inherent in GNN-based models. Moreover, existing approaches underutilize the reasoning capabilities and extensive knowledge of large language models (LLMs) in addressing these limitations. To bridge this gap, we propose two new modules. First, Preference-informed Edge Reweighting (PER) module assigns signed edge weights to qualitatively distinguish significant player interests and disinterests while then quantitatively measuring preference strength to mitigate over-smoothing in graph convolutions. Second, Preference-informed Representation Generation (PRG) module leverages LLMs to generate contextualized descriptions of games and players by reasoning personal preferences from comparing global and personal interests, thereby refining representations of players and games. Experiments on \textcolor{black}{two Steam datasets} demonstrate CPGRec+'s superior accuracy and diversity over state-of-the-art models. The code is accessible at https://github.com/HsipingLi/CPGRec-Plus.

</details>


### [184] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec是一个新颖的视频游戏推荐框架，通过三个模块（准确性驱动、多样性驱动和综合模块）平衡推荐准确性和多样性，在Steam数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频游戏推荐系统存在三个主要问题：1) 过度强调准确性而忽视多样性，导致推荐内容单一；2) 缺乏严格的游戏间连接机制来提升准确性；3) 现有多样性方法在邻居建模和消息传播中未能充分利用游戏类别和流行度等关键信息。

Method: 提出CPGRec框架，包含三个模块：1) 准确性驱动模块：通过更严格的游戏连接方式增强现有准确性导向方法；2) 多样性驱动模块：在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居，并利用流行游戏节点放大长尾游戏在玩家-游戏二分图中的影响力；3) 综合模块：结合前两个模块，采用新的负样本评分重加权方法来平衡准确性和多样性。

Result: 在Steam数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在提升游戏推荐效果方面具有有效性。数据集和源代码已匿名发布在GitHub上。

Conclusion: CPGRec框架成功解决了视频游戏推荐中准确性与多样性的平衡问题，通过创新的模块化设计和信息利用策略，为游戏推荐系统提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [185] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: U-GLAD：一种不确定性感知的生成式学习路径推荐框架，通过高斯LSTM建模认知状态分布，结合目标导向概念编码和扩散模型生成个性化学习路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习路径推荐方法存在两个主要问题：1）未能考虑历史交互的不确定性（如侥幸猜对或偶然失误）；2）缺乏对不同学习目标的适应性。这导致推荐结果存在偏差且不够个性化。

Method: 提出U-GLAD框架：1）使用高斯LSTM将学习者的认知状态建模为概率分布，捕捉潜在真实状态；2）采用目标导向概念编码器，通过多头注意力和目标特定变换动态对齐概念语义与个人学习目标；3）使用生成式扩散模型预测下一个最优概念的潜在表示，而非传统的判别式排序方法。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛评估表明，U-GLAD显著优于代表性基线方法。进一步分析证实了其在感知交互不确定性和提供稳定、目标驱动的推荐路径方面的卓越能力。

Conclusion: U-GLAD通过不确定性建模、目标自适应编码和生成式扩散方法，有效解决了学习路径推荐中的表示偏差和个性化不足问题，为个性化教育提供了更精准、稳定的推荐框架。

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [186] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SF-UBM：一种语义增强的联邦用户行为建模方法，用于解决非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐问题，通过自然语言作为桥梁连接不同领域，同时保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统面临用户数据稀疏限制，跨域推荐方法通常假设领域重叠且可访问，缺乏对隐私保护跨域推荐（PPCDR）的关注，特别是在非重叠领域设置下的三大挑战：跨域对齐困难、数据模态异构、协同过滤信号与LLM特征空间不匹配。

Method: 提出SF-UBM方法：1）使用自然语言作为通用桥梁，通过语义增强联邦架构连接不相交领域，加密共享基于文本的物品表示，用户数据保持本地；2）设计事实-反事实知识蒸馏模块，整合领域无关与领域特定知识；3）将预学习用户偏好和跨域物品表示投影到软提示空间，对齐行为与语义空间。

Result: 在三个真实世界领域对上进行了广泛实验，结果显示SF-UBM相比现有最先进方法具有显著有效性。

Conclusion: SF-UBM成功解决了非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐的三大挑战，通过语义增强联邦架构、知识蒸馏和空间对齐技术，实现了在保护用户隐私前提下的有效跨域推荐。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local. To handle Challenge 2, we design a Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation module to integrate domain-agnostic knowledge with domain-specific knowledge, across different data modalities. To tackle Challenge 3, we project pre-learned user preferences and cross-domain item representations into the soft prompt space, aligning behavioral and semantic spaces for effective LLM learning. We conduct extensive experiments on three pairs of real-world domains, and the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of SF-UBM compared to the recent SOTA methods.

</details>


### [187] [Well Begun is Half Done: Training-Free and Model-Agnostic Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14839)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Jianheng Tang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SG-URInit方法，通过整合用户交互物品的模态特征和对应簇的全局特征，为多模态推荐中的用户表示提供语义丰富的初始化，弥补用户与物品表示之间的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐方法忽视了用户表示初始化的关键作用。物品有丰富的模态信息进行初始化，而用户表示通常随机初始化，导致两者之间存在显著的语义鸿沟，影响推荐性能。

Method: 提出SG-URInit方法：1）整合用户交互物品的模态特征（局部语义）；2）结合对应簇的全局特征（全局语义）；3）训练无关且模型无关，可无缝集成到现有多模态推荐模型中，不增加训练计算开销。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，将SG-URInit集成到先进多模态推荐模型中能显著提升推荐性能。此外，SG-URInit还能进一步缓解物品冷启动问题，并加速模型收敛。

Conclusion: SG-URInit通过语义保证的用户表示初始化，有效弥补了多模态推荐中用户与物品表示之间的语义鸿沟，是一种高效实用的解决方案，能提升推荐性能、缓解冷启动问题并加速收敛。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal recommendations, which leverage diverse modality information to mitigate data sparsity and improve recommendation accuracy, have gained significant attention. However, existing multimodal recommendations overlook the critical role of user representation initialization. Unlike items, which are naturally associated with rich modality information, users lack such inherent information. Consequently, item representations initialized based on meaningful modality information and user representations initialized randomly exhibit a significant semantic gap.
  To this end, we propose a Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization (SG-URInit). SG-URInit constructs the initial representation for each user by integrating both the modality features of the items they have interacted with and the global features of their corresponding clusters. SG-URInit enables the initialization of semantically enriched user representations that effectively capture both local (item-level) and global (cluster-level) semantics.
  Our SG-URInit is training-free and model-agnostic, meaning it can be seamlessly integrated into existing multimodal recommendation models without incurring any additional computational overhead during training. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that incorporating SG-URInit into advanced multimodal recommendation models significantly enhances recommendation performance. Furthermore, the results show that SG-URInit can further alleviate the item cold-start problem and also accelerate model convergence, making it an efficient and practical solution for multimodal recommendations.

</details>


### [188] [GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14878)
*Yanyan Zou,Junbo Qi,Lunsong Huang,Yu Li,Kewei Xu,Jiabao Gao,Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Sulong Xu,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenRec是一个面向偏好的生成式推荐框架，通过页面级NTP任务、非对称线性Token合并器和GRPO-SR强化学习方法解决生成式检索在大规模工业系统中的三大挑战，在JD App上实现了显著的在线性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式检索（GR）扩展到大规模工业推荐系统面临三个关键挑战：1）分页请求机制下相同模型输入产生不一致输出；2）基于语义ID的多token项目表示编码长用户行为序列成本过高；3）生成策略与细微用户偏好信号的对齐问题。

Method: 提出GenRec框架，包含三个核心技术：1）页面级NTP任务，监督整个交互页面而非单个项目；2）非对称线性Token合并器，压缩多token语义ID同时保持全分辨率解码；3）GRPO-SR强化学习方法，结合组相对策略优化和NLL正则化，使用混合奖励（密集奖励模型+相关性门控）防止奖励攻击。

Result: 在JD App上为期一个月的在线A/B测试中，GenRec相比现有流水线实现了9.5%的点击量提升和8.7%的交易量提升，输入长度减少约2倍且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: GenRec成功解决了生成式检索在大规模工业推荐系统中的关键挑战，通过统一的仅解码器架构实现了显著的在线性能提升，证明了页面级监督、高效输入压缩和偏好对齐强化学习的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.

</details>


### [189] [Metric-agnostic Learning-to-Rank via Boosting and Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15101)
*Camilo Gomez,Pengyang Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的列表式学习排序框架，通过可微排序损失函数解决传统方法对单一排序指标的依赖问题，实现了更高效和泛化性更强的排序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习排序方法存在两个主要问题：1）直接优化排序指标（如NDCG、MAP）本质上是非平滑的，导致训练过程不稳定且低效；2）依赖单一指标优化限制了模型在其他相关排序指标上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一种新的列表式学习排序框架，包含：1）新的可微排序损失函数，结合了排序算子的平滑近似和每个查询的平均均方损失；2）采用梯度提升机来最小化针对每个列表提出的损失函数。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，该方法在信息检索指标上优于当前最先进的方法，同时保持了相似的效率。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功解决了传统学习排序方法的非可微性和泛化性限制问题，通过可微损失函数和梯度提升机实现了更高效和通用的排序模型。

Abstract: Learning-to-Rank (LTR) is a supervised machine learning approach that constructs models specifically designed to order a set of items or documents based on their relevance or importance to a given query or context. Despite significant success in real-world information retrieval systems, current LTR methods rely on one prefix ranking metric (e.g., such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) or Mean Average Precision (MAP)) for optimizing the ranking objective function. Such metric-dependent setting limits LTR methods from two perspectives: (1) non-differentiable problem: directly optimizing ranking functions over a given ranking metric is inherently non-smooth, making the training process unstable and inefficient; (2) limited ranking utility: optimizing over one single metric makes it difficult to generalize well to other ranking metrics of interest. To address the above issues, we propose a novel listwise LTR framework for efficient and generalizable ranking purpose. Specifically, we propose a new differentiable ranking loss that combines a smooth approximation to the ranking operator with the average mean square loss per query. Then, we adapt gradient-boosting machines to minimize our proposed loss with respect to each list, a novel contribution. Finally, extensive experimental results confirm that our method outperforms the current state-of-the-art in information retrieval measures with similar efficiency.

</details>
